Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [74] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种修正的heavy-ball Q-learning方法，证明了其收敛性，并识别出理论上比标准Q-learning更快收敛的条件。该方法被扩展到线性函数逼近的Q-learning，同样建立了收敛性和加速结论。分析基于Q-learning算法的切换线性系统表示及相关切换族的联合谱半径，这一视角为理解heavy-ball动量如何加速Q-learning提供了新的框架和见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning方法收敛速度有限，heavy-ball动量方法虽被引入但缺乏严格的理论收敛性证明和加速条件分析，作者希望从切换线性系统视角建立理论基础。

Method: 提出修正的heavy-ball Q-learning算法；利用切换线性系统表示Q-learning算法，通过联合谱半径分析收敛性和加速条件；将方法扩展到线性函数逼近场景。

Result: 证明了修正heavy-ball Q-learning的收敛性；识别出比标准Q-learning加速的理论条件；在表格型和线性函数逼近两类场景下均建立了收敛和加速的理论保证。

Conclusion: 切换线性系统视角为理解heavy-ball动量加速Q-learning提供了新框架，该分析框架对更广泛的RL算法分析具有参考价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [75] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAUN和RA-IPS方法，分别通过跨头注意力机制和鲁棒对抗逆倾向得分来解决 uplift modeling 中的组间相似性利用与未观测混杂偏差问题，在多个数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uplift modeling（提升建模）在估计个体处理效应（ITE）时面临两大挑战：1) 如何灵活利用处理组与对照组间的相似性来增强判别能力；2) 在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下进行去偏。

Method: 1) CHAUN：采用共享特征嵌入（shared feature embeddings）和跨头注意力机制（cross-head attention），动态整合处理特定表示与对照特定表示，增强组间相关性建模。2) RA-IPS：在约束不确定性集内对抗性优化倾向权重（propensity weights），以缓解未观测变量带来的偏差。同时从理论上证明了在已知真实倾向得分时，即便存在未观测混杂因素，ITE仍然可识别。

Result: 在CRITEO-UPLIFT、LAZADA以及生产电商数据集上，CHAUN相比最先进的 uplift 模型在QINI得分上最高提升25.6%；RA-IPS在未观测混杂场景下比标准IPS提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的CHAUN和RA-IPS方法在真实因果推断任务中有效，能够同时增强组间建模与处理未观测混杂偏差，具有显著的实践价值。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [76] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN在流体动力学代理建模中的性能，发现KAN在预测翼型表面压力分布方面表现良好，但总体性能略逊于MLP，最佳性能由GNN实现，尽管训练时间更长。KAN模型复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定且高度依赖超参数优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于KAN是否在符号回归、通用机器学习、自然语言处理和计算机视觉等领域优于MLP存在争议，作者希望评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模这一特定领域的表现。

Method: 以亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布预测为任务，对比KAN、MLP和GNN三种模型在流体动力学代理建模中的性能。

Result: KAN能有效预测压力系数并在马赫数和攻角间插值，但性能略逊于MLP；GNN性能最优但训练时间最长；KAN模型复杂度最低、训练最快，但存在训练不稳定问题，且高度依赖超参数优化。

Conclusion: KAN在流体动力学代理建模中并未展现出优于MLP的性能，其训练不稳定性和对超参数的敏感性是主要限制因素。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [77] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了fTNN，一种确定性的张量神经网络子空间方法，用于求解有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题（分数阶泊松方程和时间依赖的分数阶对流扩散方程）。该方法采用几何自适应积分分裂，将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和解析外远场三部分，并通过高斯-雅可比求积、高斯求积和确定性角度求积实现完全确定性的积分框架。为准确解析低正则性解，构造了包含显式边界特征的边界奇异性感知试验函数，并设计了两种自动选择主导指数和评估损失函数的策略。对于时间依赖问题，设计了时空可分离神经网络，将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，并结合交替神经网络子空间优化策略进行高效训练。数值实验表明，该方法在测试基准上达到高精度，特别是在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题中显著优于现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶拉普拉斯算子在有界域上的数值求解面临两大挑战：一是分数阶算子的非局部性和奇异核导致传统数值方法计算成本高昂；二是解的低正则性（边界奇异性）使得标准神经网络方法难以准确逼近。现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法在精度和计算效率上存在不足，尤其是在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟场景下。

Method: 1) 几何自适应积分分裂：根据空间依赖的近场半径将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和解析外远场三部分；2) 确定性求积框架：奇异径向积分用高斯-雅可比求积，规则径向积分用高斯求积，角度变量用确定性角度求积；3) 边界奇异性感知试验函数：构造包含显式边界特征的函数来增强对低正则性解的逼近能力；4) 主导指数自动选择策略：基于分数阶算子或联合源项导致的奇异性结构自动选择主导指数；5) 时空可分离神经网络：将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，结合交替子空间优化策略进行训练。

Result: 在测试基准上达到了高精度，特别是在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题中，性能显著优于现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的fTNN框架为分数阶拉普拉斯问题的数值求解提供了一种高效、高精度的确定性替代方案，有效处理了边界奇异性问题，并在长时间模拟中展现出优越的稳定性。该方法扩展了神经网络子空间方法在分数阶PDE求解中的应用范围。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [78] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型辅助抽样的梯度估计框架，将小批量梯度下降视为有限总体抽样问题，利用辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验场景中提升了优化性能，尤其在动量优化器下能以约一半训练轮次达到更好的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量梯度估计存在方差问题，影响收敛稳定性、速度和泛化能力。现有方差缩减技术（如SVRG、SAG）和自适应优化器虽能缓解梯度噪声，但可能引入额外计算开销。作者希望将机器学习优化与调查抽样理论相结合，从抽样角度重新审视梯度估计问题，以构建更高效的梯度估计方法。

Method: 提出模型辅助抽样框架，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量。通过引入辅助梯度预测模型，构建更高效的梯度估计器，均匀采样作为无辅助信息时的特例。该框架可轻松集成到现有优化器中，不改变其动力学特性。

Result: 在合成数据和六个基准数据集上，71-86%的实验场景中取得性能提升，尤其适用于中等输入空间。与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，所提估计器能在约一半训练轮次内实现更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 模型辅助抽样框架有效桥接了机器学习优化与调查抽样理论，在降低梯度估计方差方面表现优越，且对现有优化器兼容性好，是一种实用且高效的梯度估计改进方法。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [79] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于5.4万余条FDA医疗设备召回记录，提出了RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，可同时预测召回严重等级（三类）和根因类别（9类），在严重性预测上准确率达0.963，宏F1为0.856，ROC-AUC为0.974，多任务设置下显著优于单任务基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多分别处理召回事件预测或根因分析，缺乏对召回严重等级与根因类别的联合建模；同时，日益增长的FDA召回记录给事后召回分类、严重性评估和根因解读带来挑战，亟需自动化框架支持。

Method: 使用54,165条openFDA召回记录（2002-2025年），首先评估经典机器学习和Boosting模型；随后开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，结合PubMedBERT文本表征召回叙述与嵌入表征结构化分类特征（产品代码、规章编号、医疗专业），同时预测严重等级（I/II/III类）和根因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，基于LightGBM的文本-表格配置取得最优：准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线；模型风险排名与观测根因严重模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的事后召回分类、监管决策辅助及基于模型的根因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [80] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好反馈学习势能函数，用于基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS），在保持最优策略集不变的前提下提升稀疏奖励环境中的样本效率并防止奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励环境中缺乏中间反馈，智能体难以进行探索和信用分配；朴素奖励塑形易导致奖励黑客行为；现有的PBRS需要专家设计势能函数，成本高且难以推广。

Method: 使用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好查询，基于偏好标签训练势能函数模型，将该势能函数用于PBRS以塑形原始奖励，从而在保持最优策略集不变的同时加速学习。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中验证了方法的有效性，证明即使小VLM的偏好标签精度有限，仍能提升样本效率；揭示了VLM偏好标签准确率与样本效率改进之间的关联。

Conclusion: 首次将VLM偏好学习用于合成PBRS势能函数，提供了一种低成本、原则性的解决方案，能有效提升样本效率并增强对奖励黑客行为的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [81] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于归一化相关度量（NRM）框架的全信息流归因方法，能够追踪事件时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）中通过事件诱导变量的完整信息路径，克服了现有解释方法仅关注部分信息流的局限性，并在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅追踪事件相关嵌入到输出的贡献，忽略了通过事件诱导变量的信息路径，而这些变量在捕捉长时依赖中起核心作用。

Method: 基于NRM框架，量化来自事件嵌入以及通过事件诱导变量的信息流；通过模块化分解程序处理ETGNN的架构复杂性，系统构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构。

Result: 在传染病追踪和社会动态两个合成数据集以及一个真实政治事件网络数据集上，该方法在定性和定量实验中均一致优于现有解释方法，并生成更可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 所提出的全信息流归因方法能够更全面地解释ETGNN，通过考虑事件诱导变量的信息路径，在解释质量和可解释性上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [82] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了图神经网络（GNN）从表示学习技术到默认关系数据模型的演进过程，围绕统一设计空间从第一性原理推导谱域与空域方法，连接表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次，并在12个应用领域中分析图结构收益与成本，揭示了异质性、规模效应、时间动态等跨领域共性挑战，以及过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性等实际部署约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答"图结构何时值得计算成本、何时不值得"这一核心问题，系统评估GNN在不同领域的实际增益与伪增益（弱基线或分裂方式带来的假象），为从业者提供架构选择与部署的决策依据。

Method: 围绕统一设计空间（design space），从共享第一性原理推导谱域与空域两种图神经网络形式，结合Weisfeiler-Leman层次分析表达能力，对12个应用领域分别指定图构建方式与成本、主导架构家族及其原因、区分报告增益来自真正改进还是弱基线或数据分裂假象，最后进行跨领域对比提取共性规律。

Result: 识别出跨领域共性模式：异质性与规模效应几乎在所有领域削弱相同模型、时序图始终比静态图更难、公开排行榜领先架构很少被实际部署使用。过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性、可解释性不是可选的收尾清单，而是决定模型能否被采纳的硬约束。

Conclusion: GNN的实际价值因领域和图结构选择而异，领域特定约束（异质性、时间动态、鲁棒性等）是决定部署的关键因素，提示研究应更关注真实场景约束而非排行榜指标。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [83] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共享词汇表的LLM家族（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中无监督源任务选择问题，本文提出FisherSketch方法。理论表明在共享输出头设定下，表征相似性指标（如CKA）不可识别迁移性。Fisher对齐等价于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入的余弦，FisherSketch通过单次流式估计实现高效计算（16KB任务签名+192KB流状态），无需物化Fisher矩阵。实验验证其在Llama-3.1-8B等模型上优于激活相似性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学字符串领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列），存在多个共享词汇表（tokenizer）但预测目标不同的候选语料库，需要无训练（training-free）的源任务选择方法。然而，表征相似性指标在缺乏标签条件误差几何假设时信息量不足，而经典的更新几何指标在词汇表规模下计算代价过高。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明共享输出头设定下表征相似性（如CKA）对迁移不可识别——模型可具有相同表征但头部更新正交。2. 核心恒等式：证明头部Fisher对齐等价于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦值，揭示了激活因子、误差因子和耦合因子。3. FisherSketch算法：通过单次流式传输直接估计该余弦，生成16KB任务签名（m=4096）和192KB每任务流状态，无需物化完整的Fisher矩阵。

Result: FisherSketch使得K=128,256的头部Fisher对齐实用化，存储开销小（可存放在模型哈希旁）。在共享参数验证、内部层验证以及Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer偏移实验中，FisherSketch在激活相似性无法区分任务时仍能保持信息量。

Conclusion: FisherSketch不仅可用于源任务选择，其产生的签名和边际量还可作为诊断工具，研究LLM任务相似性究竟由激活、误差还是二者的耦合驱动。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。通过将判别器设置为二次能量形式，证明了随机微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程。稳定性分析揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关性可将弱方向提升至可学习阈值以上，而过强的相关性会破坏恢复。数值模拟和真实数据集实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前可解的GAN分析假设无条件信号且潜在协方差为对角阵，缺乏对类别相关、相关性和非零均值潜在结构的研究。本文旨在扩展多特征判别器设置，探索结构化潜在协方差对GAN训练动力学的影响。

Method: 采用线性生成器和二次能量判别器的可解高维GAN模型，通过随机过程的高维极限分析得到确定性常微分方程；在匹配协方差特化下进行模式分解的稳定性分析，推导可学习区间；结合数值模拟和真实数据集实验验证。

Result: 所有异构性通过概率加权有效二阶矩进入动力学；学习速率和噪声水平决定了模式可解区间；低秩相关性能提升弱方向的可学习性，过强相关性则破坏恢复；实验表明信息性生成器协方差改善与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

Conclusion: 建立了结构化潜在协方差下GAN训练动力学的一般理论框架，揭示了信号增强机制，为理解GAN中协方差设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [85] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖远距离关系中的心理健康困扰及关系触发因素。研究在多重标签疾病分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三个任务上基准测试了7个微调Transformer模型和5个大语言模型，发现Claude-3-Haiku在疾病分类上最优（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最强（Macro-F1=0.519），并揭示了焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NLP心理健康建模通常将心理疾病视为孤立现象，忽略了人际交往背景。本研究旨在通过远距离关系这一特定人际情境，捕捉心理健康困扰及其关系触发因素，推动从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。

Method: 收集Reddit远距离关系相关帖子，由精神科医生进行多维度标注（诊断类别、关系压力触发因素、关系阶段）；在三个任务（多标签疾病分类、关系触发检测、时间阶段预测）上评估7个微调Transformer模型和5个大语言模型。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在疾病分类任务上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最优（Macro-F1=0.519）；不同模型家族在任务上存在明显能力差异；发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系语境的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模范式转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [86] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从Instagram和Reddit收集的博彩广告数据集，并进行了操纵性和欺骗性广告实践的人工标注，包括分类标签和人工解释，旨在检测和分析博彩广告中的操纵性策略及其对用户心理健康的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管社交媒体上博彩应用的推广显著增加，且这些广告可能使用误导性技巧影响用户心理健康，但缺乏公开可用的带注释数据集来支持自动检测操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的研究。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交平台收集博彩相关广告，通过人工标注操纵性和欺骗性广告实践，提供分类标签和人类推理解释，并分析常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响。

Result: 构建了一个包含人工注释的博彩广告数据集，涵盖分类标签和解释性推理，并识别了博彩广告中常用的操纵性策略。

Conclusion: 该框架可支持实际应用，如警告用户的浏览器插件和帮助监管机构监测在线博彩广告的自动化网络爬虫。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [87] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战时间序列预测中“更大模型带来更好精度”的主流观点，通过调优岭回归的预处理（上下文长度、归一化、正则化、数据增强），在8个标准基准上超越了许多Transformer/MLP/CNN基线模型，并揭示了数据本身的结构特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究倾向于使用越来越大的模型架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型），但作者认为大部分精度提升可以通过更低成本的预处理调优实现，而非单纯扩大模型规模。

Method: 以岭回归（Ridge regression）作为测试平台（因其闭式解和可解释权重），在8个标准基准上搜索超参数：上下文长度、局部归一化（学习到的尾部比例而非全部上下文）、正则化强度、数据增强。对比了Transformer、MLP、CNN等基线模型。

Result: （1）最优回溯长度高度依赖于序列，且与预测步长呈非单调关系，拟合幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19，挑战了“更长预测需要更长历史”的惯例；（2）对上下文的学习尾部部分而非全部进行归一化几乎普遍更优；（3）同一数据集的序列间超参数最佳共享程度各异。调优后的岭回归模型在大多数数据集-预测步长组合上击败了先前线性模型，并在8个基准中的6个上超越Transformer/MLP/CNN基线。

Conclusion: 预处理调优可以在远低于大模型的成本下缩小大部分性能差距；优化的超参数也可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大型模型默默吸收到其学习参数中的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [88] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了控制ODE的可辨识性界限，并推导了样本复杂度下界，量化了从多解观测中可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中，从观测解数据学习控制方程是一个基础挑战，但理论上关于真实ODE能否从多解观测中被唯一且稳定辨识的条件尚不完善，且缺乏对样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量；建立从线性ODE到Lipschitz/Hölder连续向量场的非线性类等广泛结构方程的可辨识性界限；利用该度量推导相关ODE类的度量熵估计并分析样本复杂度。

Result: 给出了不同ODE类（从线性到非线性）的可辨识性条件，量化了从解数据区分不同方程所需的精度；推导了样本复杂度上界，明确了可靠恢复控制方程所需的观测数。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离是衡量微分方程辨识问题的自然度量，能编码极小极大结构；本文填补了ODE辨识理论条件的空白，为样本复杂度提供了首个定量分析。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [89] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RLAIF的端到端框架，用于生成可迁移的求职搜索查询。研究发现，优化器的选择对性能影响不大，关键在于奖励塑造；GRPO对虚假奖励信号特别敏感，而引入基于规则的奖励下限可有效缓解奖励破解问题。训练时奖励模型会将性能增益膨胀2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统求职搜索平台使用低带宽查询接口，难以捕捉求职者档案的高维复杂性，亟需一种能生成可迁移、去身份化但保留通用资质的搜索查询方法。

Method: 采用RLAIF（从AI反馈中强化学习）框架，并系统对比不同优化器（RLOO、REINFORCE++、GRPO），引入基于规则的确定性奖励下限来修正奖励破解行为。

Result: 无评论家优化器的性能主要由奖励塑造决定，算法选择几乎无关紧要；GRPO的组相对优势归一化使其对虚假奖励信号特别敏感；引入奖励下限后跨族评估法官质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将性能增益膨胀2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上取决于奖励塑造纪律的执行，而非优化器的选择；GRPO在对抗性奖励面上存在固有脆弱性。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [90] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，用于板状结构中损伤的精准定位与尺寸评估，在计算效率和预测精度上均优于传统CNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署受限于标注实验数据稀缺以及大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本高昂。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大量合成数据进行预训练，通过卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习，结合前馈神经网络，并利用少量实验数据进行迁移学习微调。

Result: 损伤定位R²>0.93，损伤尺寸评估R²>0.99，显著优于CNN基线方法，并对未见过的损伤场景展现出良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架是准确、计算高效且实用的真实世界导波结构健康监测解决方案。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [91] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明梯度均衡（GEQ）在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性，进而等价于遗憾最小化和校准等经典在线学习框架，并建立了GEQ与约束/无约束决策集之间的约简关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEQ作为新兴在线优化框架与遗憾最小化等经典框架的关系尚不明确，先前工作表明两者误差指标不可比，需要精确定位GEQ在在线学习全景中的位置。

Method: 通过构造双向黑箱约简：证明任何Blackwell可逼近问题可用GEQ预言机求解且无渐进损失，反之亦然；进而利用可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准的已知等价性建立GEQ与这些框架的等价关系。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性在算法意义上等价，从而与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；给出了GEQ的充要条件；建立了约束与无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念间的约简。

Conclusion: GEQ可归入经典在线学习框架体系，其约简可迁移乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证，为GEQ的理论分析和应用提供更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k SAE架构兼容的稀疏正则化方法（off-support ℓ1惩罚和尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），在多个视觉基础模型上验证了硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化具有互补性，能提升单义性且不损害重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Top-k SAE虽通过激活函数实现架构稀疏性，避免了ℓ1惩罚的已知缺陷，但仍存在固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度、易过拟合到训练时k值等问题。作者旨在探索硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化是否能够互补，而非互斥。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种正则化：(1) off-support ℓ1惩罚——对未被Top-k选中的单元施加ℓ1惩罚；(2) 尺度不变ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚——将代码集中到更少的有效单元。两种惩罚仅应用于批次活跃单元（batch-active units），即批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化均一致提升单义性，且不损害重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的隐变量上，使重建对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并改善小预算线性探测性能。

Conclusion: 硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化是互补的而非互斥的，这一发现为改进SAE设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [93] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过MMBench2数据集发现世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，并提出了基于覆盖感知的检测与缓解方法，仅需50条真实轨迹即可微调预训练模型适应全新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的可控未来帧，但频繁出现幻觉——视觉上流畅但偏离真实动力学。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，且轻量级数据驱动信号既能检测幻觉也能指导缓解。

Method: 构建427小时、210任务的MMBench2数据集，训练350M参数世界模型；识别三种幻觉模式（感知型、动作边缘化型、场景发散型），开发三种预测信号；提出覆盖感知采样技术（训练时）和基于幻觉预测器的好奇心奖励（在线）来闭合覆盖缺口。

Result: 在线数据收集仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可高效微调预训练世界模型，使其适应完全未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的同一组信号也可用于缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [94] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过自回归建模框架替代基于流的玻尔兹曼生成器，克服了归一化流在可逆性约束和计算成本上的局限，在分子平衡态采样任务上取得显著提升，并训练了1.32亿参数的Robin模型，在零样本能量误差上降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流，但离散时间流受严格可逆性约束导致表达性有限，连续时间流则需昂贵的似然计算，限制了模型的可扩展性和性能。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代流模型，规避流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的顺序干预，并利用在大语言模型中有效的架构提升可扩展性。

Result: ArBG在所有基准测试中显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在更大的肽系统（如10残基的Chignolin）上表现突出。Robin模型（1.32亿参数）在8残基系统上零样本能量误差E-W₂降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归建模框架是玻尔兹曼生成器的一个有前景的新范式，能够有效克服流的局限性，在分子平衡态采样任务上实现更优性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [95] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励（基于执行反馈）训练LLM，无需真实答案即可优化基于分数的任务，并在精确求解基准上实现迁移提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案来分配奖励，限制了其在真实解未知的任务（如基于分数的优化问题）中的应用。作者希望消除对真实答案的依赖，仅靠执行反馈（分数）来有效训练LLM。

Method: 提出Ranking-induced VERifiable framework (RiVER)，包含两个关键设计：1) 实例级别的奖励校准（calibrated reward shaping），解决规模主导问题；2) 强调排名靠前的求解者（top-ranked solvers），同时对其他有效解保留有界反馈，解决频率主导问题。

Result: 在12个AtCoder Heuristic Contest任务上训练，RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE-Bench上分别提升8.9%和9.4%的评级排名；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上平均绝对提升2.4%和3.5%。而使用原始执行分数的基线仅在ALE上提升，无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务配合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实答案的情况下作为训练环境，有效提升LLM的通用编码能力，包括精确求解任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Reducing Redundancy in Whole-Slide Image Patching for Scalable Indexing and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26157)
*Jialiang Geng,Ghazal Alabtah,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ARReST是一种基于拮抗冗余缩减的策略，通过识别并剪枝跨类别区分贡献最小的病理切片块，在不影响检索性能的前提下实现WSI索引压缩3%-60%，为下一代检索驱动的临床AI系统提供可扩展、低成本的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理学的快速发展对全切片图像（WSI）的索引与检索提出了迫切需求，尤其在检索增强生成（RAG）等生成式AI工作流中，可靠的相似性搜索对临床决策至关重要。然而高性能存储的高昂成本限制了WSI索引的可扩展性和可及性，因此需要一种在保持检索精度的同时降低存储需求的方法。

Method: 提出ARReST（拮抗冗余缩减策略），这是一个基于原则的对立框架，利用不同组织类别间的冗余来减少每个WSI中需要索引的切片块数量。不同于仅消除类内重复，ARReST识别并剪枝那些对跨类别区分贡献最小的拮抗切片块（antithetical patches），从而压缩可搜索档案。通过最小化多余切片块表示，减少存储占用、降低计算开销并加速相似性搜索。

Result: 在TCGA（涵盖21个器官的癌症基因组图谱）数据集上的实验表明，ARReST在保持竞争性检索性能的同时实现了显著的索引压缩。存储节省可达3%到60%（平均14%±13%），且对许多器官而言不会损害检索性能。

Conclusion: 该策略实现了可扩展、成本高效的WSI索引，特别适用于下一代检索驱动的临床AI系统。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital pathology has created an urgent need for efficient indexing and retrieval of whole slide images (WSIs). This need is intensified by emerging generative AI workflows, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which require dependable similarity search to support high-stakes clinical decision-making. Yet the substantial cost of high-performance storage limits the scalability and accessibility of WSI indexing for many healthcare institutions. Consequently, methods that can reduce storage demands while preserving retrieval accuracy have become a critical research priority. We propose ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy), a principled oppositional framework that leverages redundancy across dissimilar tissue classes to markedly decrease the number of patches that must be indexed from each WSI. Instead of eliminating only within-class duplicates, ARReST identifies antithetical patches-those whose representations contribute minimally to cross-class discrimination-and prunes them from the searchable archive. This targeted reduction substantially compresses the index without sacrificing morphological diversity or retrieval fidelity. By minimizing superfluous patch representations, ARReST reduces storage footprint, lowers computational overhead, and accelerates similarity search across large pathology repositories. Extensive experiments on TCGA repository (The Cancer Genome Atlas with 21 organs) demonstrate that ARReST achieves significant index compression while maintaining competitive retrieval performance. The observed storage savings of 3% to 60% (14%$\pm$13%) can be reliably achieved without compromising retrieval performance for many organs. The proposed strategy enables scalable, cost-efficient WSI indexing and is well-suited for next-generation retrieval-driven clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [97] [From Clicks to Intent: Cross-Platform Session Embeddings with LLM-Distilled Taxonomy for Financial Services Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26277)
*Dianjing Fan,Yao Li,Kyaw Hpone Myint,Dwipam Katariya,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的双重意图预测框架，利用自监督Transformer将网络点击流编码为会话嵌入，同时结合LLM生成可解释的意图标签，在金融服务的跨渠道个性化推荐中，分别提升了移动端首页排序1.88%的Recall@1和降低了13.38%的Log Loss，并在用户转化预测任务上比纯LLM标签方法高出4.3%的micro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务业中，登录前的网页交互与登录后的应用内体验差异巨大，跨渠道实体解析（如将匿名网页会话与已认证的移动账户匹配）存在挑战，导致网页端的意图信号在后认证个性化中未被充分利用。现有方法缺乏灵活性，无法同时支持定量的下游推荐和大规模的定性理解。

Method: 提出可扩展的双重意图预测框架：1）自监督Transformer将多模态点击流编码为紧凑的会话嵌入；2）基于LLM的标签生成与蒸馏管道产生可解释的意图标签。

Result: 在移动端首页排序任务中，会话嵌入相比生产基线提升Recall@1 1.88%，降低Log Loss 13.38%；在用户转化预测任务中，嵌入比LLM标签高4.3% micro F1，蒸馏层以仅7%的性能代价实现超低延迟的可解释标签。

Conclusion: 自监督点击流表示结合LLM蒸馏的标签体系，可以同时在定量任务和定性理解上服务于生产环境中的个性化推荐。

Abstract: Sequential user behavior modeling is widely adopted in industrial recommender systems; however, significant gaps remain in financial services, where pre-login web interactions and authenticated in-app experiences differ drastically. Specifically, pre-login web users typically explore new products, whereas logged-in app users focus on account servicing. Due to the challenge of cross-channel entity resolution (e.g., matching anonymous web sessions to authenticated mobile accounts), web-based intent signals remain underutilized for post-authentication personalization. Existing methods for capturing web-based intent are often ad-hoc and narrow, lacking the flexibility to support both quantitative downstream recommendations and qualitative understanding at scale. In this work, we propose a scalable and dual-purpose intent prediction framework for web-based interactions and demonstrate its applicability for personalization. Our approach transforms raw web clickstreams into two outputs: a self-supervised Transformer encodes multi-modal clickstreams into a compact session embedding, while an LLM-based taxonomy generation and distillation pipeline produces interpretable intent labels. Our system demonstrates that self-supervised clickstream representations combined with LLM-distilled taxonomies can jointly serve quantitative tasks and qualitative understanding in production: on the mobile homepage tile ranking task, the session embedding improves macro Recall@1 by 1.88% and reduces Log Loss by 13.38% over production baselines. On the user conversion prediction task, the embedding outperforms the LLM labels by 4.3% on micro F1, while the distillation layer delivers interpretable labels at ultra-low latency with only a 7% performance drop.

</details>


### [98] [Scoring Is Not Enough: Addressing Gaps in Utility-fairness Trade-offs for Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26369)
*Shubham Singh,Ian A. Kash,Mesrob I. Ohannessian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文证明，与追求效用的目标不同，评分函数在实现效用与公平性的权衡时是次优的，并提出了半贪心后处理方法作为更优的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算法公平性研究的兴起，现有方法试图通过学习评分函数来同时优化效用和公平性，但作者怀疑评分函数的表达能力不足以实现所有公平-效用权衡，因此希望从理论上严格证明这一局限性。

Method: 通过构造一系列反例，在通用公平性公式下，从确定性评分、随机化评分、单查询和多查询等多个维度证明评分函数的次优性；同时提出半贪心后处理方法，并通过实验验证其性能。

Result: 证明了无论评分函数是确定性的还是随机化的，也无论公平性是在单查询还是多查询范围内衡量，评分函数都无法达到最优的公平-效用权衡；而半贪心后处理方法能够以可接受的计算成本逼近穷举后处理的理想性能。

Conclusion: 评分函数在公平-效用权衡中本质上存在局限性，不应被视为实现公平排序的通用工具；半贪心后处理是一种实用且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Scoring functions are used to represent the relevance of individual documents. In modern information retrieval or recommendation systems, they are often learned from data and play a pivotal role in ranking sets of documents or items in a way that maximizes utility to a query or user. With the recent interest in algorithmic fairness, the success of scoring has naturally led to methods that learn scores that simultaneously trade off fairness and utility. In this work, we show that in stark contrast with utility-centric objectives, scoring is sub-optimal in achieving all utility-fairness trade-offs. We establish this with a series of counter-examples with a generic fairness formulation. We show that the issue persists whether we have a deterministic scoring function or a randomized one, or whether we measure fairness at the scope of a single query or across multiple queries. On the positive side, we empirically demonstrate that semi-greedy post-processing has the potential to achieve much better trade-offs, often approaching the ideal of exhaustive post-processing in a tractable way.

</details>


### [99] [TileMaxSim: IO-Aware GPU MaxSim Scoring with Dimension Tiling and Fused Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26439)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TileMaxSim，一种IO感知的Triton内核系列，通过多查询SRAM分块、维度分块和融合乘积量化评分，在NVIDIA H100 GPU上达到80.2%的峰值HBM带宽，实现82M文档/秒的评分速度，相比朴素实现获得220倍加速，同时保持与参考MaxSim完全相同的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型（如ColBERT）依赖细粒度token级MaxSim评分实现高精度，但现有GPU实现仅达到5-18%的峰值HBM带宽，严重受限于内存带宽瓶颈。

Method: (1) 多查询SRAM分块：将文档嵌入流式通过共享内存，在寄存器中累积每个查询token的MaxSim值，每个嵌入仅从HBM读取一次；(2) 维度分块：将嵌入维度划分为128宽的分块，处理d>128的嵌入；(3) 融合乘积量化评分：通过共享内存查找表，将HBM I/O减少约31倍。

Result: H100 GPU上达80.2%峰值HBM带宽，82M文档/秒（MS MARCO上71.6M/s），比循环评分快220倍，比融合PyTorch快6.5倍，比torch.compile快6.6-8.5倍，比WARP CPU引擎快469倍；ColBERTv2/PLAID中100K候选评分延迟从268ms降至1.2ms（端到端延迟降低98%）。

Conclusion: TileMaxSim通过IO感知的GPU内核设计，彻底解决了MaxSim评分的带宽瓶颈，在保持精确检索质量的同时实现数量级的性能提升，可作为ColBERTv2/PLAID的即插即用替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art accuracy through fine-grained token-level MaxSim scoring, yet existing GPU implementations leave most hardware performance unused. We give a roofline analysis of MaxSim on modern GPUs and identify a severe bandwidth gap: naive implementations reach only 5-18% of peak HBM bandwidth because they materialize the Nq x Nd similarity matrix, wasting memory traffic on data that is consumed once and discarded. We present TileMaxSim, a family of IO-aware Triton kernels that close this gap via (1) multi-query SRAM tiling that streams document embeddings through shared memory while accumulating per-query-token maxima in registers, reading each embedding from HBM exactly once; (2) dimension tiling that partitions the embedding dimension into 128-wide chunks, enabling scoring for d > 128 embeddings that overflow shared memory; and (3) fused product-quantization scoring via shared-memory lookup tables, cutting HBM I/O by up to ~31x. On NVIDIA H100 GPUs, TileMaxSim reaches 80.2% of peak HBM bandwidth and scores 82M documents/second (71.6M/s on real MS MARCO passages), a 220x speedup over loop-based scoring, 6.5x over fused PyTorch, 6.6-8.5x over torch.compile, and 469x the scoring throughput of WARP's CPU engine on the same node. TileMaxSim preserves exact retrieval quality: on MS MARCO and three BEIR benchmarks, rankings match reference MaxSim. As a drop-in replacement in ColBERTv2/PLAID, it cuts scoring latency at 100K candidates from 268 ms to 1.2 ms (98% lower end-to-end latency). We further show constant throughput from 100K to 500K documents, data-parallel multi-GPU sharding, robustness across dimensions 64-768, and FP16/BF16/FP32 support. Concurrent work independently develops an IO-aware fused MaxSim kernel; we differ in dimension tiling for d > 128 and fused product-quantization scoring.

</details>


### [100] [GPUSparse: GPU-Accelerated Learned Sparse Retrieval with Parallel Inverted Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26441)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GPUSparse是面向GPU加速的精确学习型稀疏检索系统，通过GPU并行倒排索引、批量散射累加评分算法和融合Triton内核，在MS MARCO段落排序上实现与CPU精确评分三位小数一致，同时带来235倍加速（1.27ms vs 298ms每查询），且吞吐量达787 QPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型稀疏检索模型（如SPLADE）在检索质量上可媲美稠密模型，但推理阶段仍依赖CPU倒排索引遍历算法（如WAND），这成为大规模实时服务的基本瓶颈。作者旨在通过GPU加速解决这一效率问题。

Method: (1) GPU并行倒排索引，采用块对齐、warp聚合的倒排列表设计；(2) 批量散射累加（scatter-add）评分算法，可同时处理数百条查询；(3) 融合Triton内核，并分析工作效率与硬件利用率之间的权衡。

Result: 在MS MARCO段落排序（880万段落）上，GPU稀疏检索精度与CPU精确评分一致（MRR@10=0.383），召回率Recall@1000≥0.999；相比Pyserini CPU实现235倍加速（1.27ms vs 298ms）；相比Seismic（最快CPU稀疏检索系统，牺牲25%召回率换取速度），GPUSparse实现精确评分且吞吐量787 QPS；文档并行内核达到H100峰值HBM带宽的62.6%。

Conclusion: GPUSparse成功将稀疏检索迁移到GPU，实现了精确评分的同时大幅提升速度，揭示了GPU稀疏检索中工作效率与带宽效率之间的根本权衡。融合内核实现相比忠实复现的SPARe迭代方案快23-270倍。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE achieve retrieval quality competitive with dense models while preserving the interpretability and exact-match advantages of sparse representations. However, inference-time scoring still relies on CPU-bound inverted index traversal algorithms (WAND, Block-Max WAND), creating a fundamental bottleneck for real-time serving at scale. We present GPUSparse, a system for GPU-accelerated exact learned sparse retrieval that introduces: (1) a GPU-parallel inverted index with block-aligned, warp-coalesced posting lists; (2) a batched scatter-add scoring algorithm that processes hundreds of queries simultaneously; and (3) fused Triton kernels with an analysis of the tradeoff between work-efficiency and hardware utilization. On MS MARCO passage ranking (8.8M passages) with real SPLADE embeddings, GPUSparse matches CPU exact scoring to three decimals (MRR@10=0.383, equal to Pyserini SPLADE at this precision; Recall@1000>=0.999 vs. dense matmul, the residual from floating-point tie-breaking) while providing a 235x speedup over Pyserini CPU at 8.8M documents (1.27ms vs. 298ms per query). Compared to Seismic (the fastest CPU sparse retrieval system), which trades 25% recall for speed (R@1000=0.738 vs. 0.983 exact), GPUSparse achieves exact scoring at 787 QPS throughput (batch 500) on the full 8.8M collection, with 1.3ms per query. Our document-parallel kernel reaches 62.6% of H100 peak HBM bandwidth, revealing a fundamental work-efficiency vs. bandwidth-efficiency tradeoff in GPU sparse retrieval. The reformulation of sparse scoring as scatter-add over an inverted index is shared with SPARe's iterative mode; our contribution is its fused-kernel realization, which we measure to be 23-270x faster than a faithful SPARe iterative reimplementation.

</details>


### [101] [Attributed, But Not Incremental: Cannibalization-Corrected Attribution for Large-Scale Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26690)
*Donghui Li,Bowen Yuan,Zili Yang,Qinxin Chen,Lijing Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对大型付费增长广告系统中归因与蚕食效应不匹配的问题，提出了一种实验校准的归因修正框架，利用增量实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏提升测量转化为每日校正估计，并在业务层级间分配校准后的蚕食量。在TikTok全球多市场部署后，蚕食率降低了约15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 付费归因的转化量（如DNU）会系统性高估真实增量增长，因为付费渠道与自然需求、品牌流量或其他渠道存在重叠，导致归因-蚕食不匹配，扭曲增量ROI测量和大规模预算决策。

Method: 以增量性实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏的提升测量值转换为每日校正估计；在结构一致性约束下，将校准后的蚕食量分配至业务层级。

Result: 离线前向验证表明，该框架相比原始归因和细粒度ML基线显著降低了校准误差；在TikTok全球多市场部署后，蚕食率降低了约15个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效校正归因偏差，改善预算分配和渠道诊断，并在实际工业系统中取得了显著的蚕食率降低效果。

Abstract: In large-scale paid acquisition and growth advertising systems, production attribution outputs are widely used for daily budget allocation and channel diagnosis. However, paid-attributed conversions such as daily new users (DNU) may systematically overstate true incremental growth when paid channels overlap with organic demand, brand-driven traffic, or other acquisition channels. This attribution-cannibalization mismatch can distort incremental ROI measurement and budget decisions at scale.
  We propose an experiment-calibrated attribution correction framework that uses incrementality experiments as causal anchors to convert sparse lift measurements into daily correction estimates. To make the corrected signal actionable at production granularity, we further allocate calibrated cannibalization volume across business hierarchies under structural consistency constraints. Offline forward-in-time validation against channel-level incrementality experiment readouts shows that the proposed framework substantially reduces calibration error relative to raw attribution and fine-grained ML baselines. Deployed across multiple global TikTok markets, the system supported budget and traffic strategy adjustments that were followed by an approximately 15-percentage-point reduction in the measured cannibalization rate.

</details>


### [102] [A Shared IPTC Topic Space for Cross-Source Topic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26845)
*Din Iskakov,Sebastian Gonçalves,Marco Idiat,Mendeli Vainstein,Aline Villavicencio,Ronaldo Menezes,Rodrigo Wilkens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的框架，通过IPTC媒体主题分类法将不同语料库映射到共享主题空间，从而实现对跨媒体主题注意力的可比分析。框架使用引导BERTopic发现主题，通过加权关键词和目标质心匹配94个IPTC一级主题，再按最大相似度规则聚合到17个父主题。在纽约时报2011语料库上，经过模型筛选、映射优化、最终比较、消融实验和阈值校准五个阶段验证，引导家族在严格阈值下比零样本基线保持更强的映射覆盖率，父主题富集目标构建改善了覆盖率和一致性，且覆盖率随阈值收紧呈渐进下降而非骤降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同媒体语料库分别拟合主题模型会产生各自特有的主题空间，无法直接对齐，阻碍了跨媒体主题注意力的比较研究。

Method: 采用引导BERTopic发现主题，通过加权关键词和目标质心计算与IPTC Media Topics中94个一级主题的相似度进行评分，再按最大相似度规则向上聚合到17个IPTC父主题。框架开发经过五个阶段的递进筛选：宽模型筛选、聚焦映射优化、严格最终比较、目标构建消融、阈值校准。

Result: 在纽约时报2011语料上，引导家族在更严格赋值阈值下比零样本基线保持更强的映射覆盖率；父主题富集的目标构建同时改善了覆盖率和父级一致性；覆盖率随阈值收紧呈渐进下降而非骤降。

Conclusion: 贡献了一种外部锚定的方法用于构建共享主题空间，能够实现可复现的跨源主题比较。

Abstract: Comparing topic attention across different media is hindered by a fundamental modelling problem: topic models fitted separately to each corpus produce corpus-specific topic spaces that cannot be aligned directly. This paper presents a reproducible framework that places corpora in a single shared topic space defined by a taxonomy. Discovered topics are obtained with guided BERTopic, scored against the ninety-four IPTC Media Topics' taxonomy topics (level-1) through weighted keyword and target centroids, and then collapsed upward to seventeen IPTC parent topics by a maximum-similarity rule. The framework was developed and selected on a controlled New York Times 2011 corpus through a narrowing sequence: a broad model screen, a focused mapping refinement, a strict finalist comparison, a target-construction ablation, and a threshold calibration. In this corpus, the guided family retained substantially stronger mapped coverage than a zero-shot benchmark under stricter assignment thresholds, a parent-enriched target construction improved both coverage and parent consistency, and coverage declined gradually rather than collapsing as the assignment threshold was tightened. The contribution is an externally anchored method for constructing a shared topic space that enables reproducible cross-source topic comparison.

</details>


### [103] [TriPAH: Imbalance-Aware Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing for Cross-Modal Medical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27010)
*Jiaming Bian,Songming Li,Yurui Song,Yunfei Chen,Yichao Cao,Jun Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了TriPAH（三提示亲和哈希框架），通过本体驱动的患者级提示生成、轻量级提示-令牌混合器以及患者级一致性模块，有效解决了跨模态医学哈希检索中的语义碎片化、长尾标签和量化脆弱性问题，在三个公开数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态医学哈希检索方法存在语义碎片化问题，原因包括临床语言噪声大、标签长尾分布以及脆弱的量化过程削弱了跨模态对齐。

Method: 1) 本体驱动的患者级提示（Ontology-grounded patient-level prompts）：基于规范化临床线索生成低噪声文本表示，实现初始对齐；2) 轻量级提示-令牌混合器（Lightweight prompt-token mixer）：执行层次化多粒度对齐，在非对称多任务目标下产生适合量化的特征，该目标耦合了多正例对比对齐、不平衡感知分类和渐进式量化正则化；3) 患者级一致性模块（Patient-level consistency module）：在互补视图间稳定哈希码。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，TriPAH显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: TriPAH通过提示工程、多粒度对齐和一致性约束等创新设计，有效解决了医学跨模态检索中的关键挑战，为大规模医学数据管理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: In the era of big medical data, efficient cross-modal retrieval is pivotal for evidence-based diagnosis and large-scale case management. Cross-modal medical hashing retrieval aims to enable efficient image-text search and support downstream tasks such as case-based reasoning and decision support by learning compact, semantically aligned binary codes. However, current methods suffer from semantic fragmentation due to noisy clinical language, long-tailed labels, and brittle quantization that weakens alignment. We propose TriPAH, a Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing framework. TriPAH synthesizes ontology-grounded, patient-level prompts conditioned on normalized clinical cues to yield low-noise textual representations for initial alignment. A lightweight prompt-token mixer performs hierarchical, multi-granularity alignment and produces quantization-ready features under an asymmetric multi-task objective coupling multi-positive contrastive alignment, imbalance-aware classification, and progressive quantization regularization. A patient-level consistency module further stabilizes codes across complementary views. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that TriPAH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [104] [UniFormer: Efficient and Unified Model-Centric Scaling for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27058)
*Bo Chen,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Ruihao Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Chenghou Jin,Qinglin Jia,Baixuan He,Hechang Pan,Yiwu Liu,Jian Liang,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniFormer，一个面向工业推荐系统的统一模型中心扩展框架，通过将建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间、引入语义分词实现请求级推理加速、多序列交叉注意力防止偏好崩溃，以及在快手和快手极速版两个场景的在线A/B测试中显著提升了用户参与度和交互指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统通过组件中心扩展提升模型能力，但缺乏统一的模型中心扩展框架，且局限于特征空间层面，无法覆盖整体建模空间。

Method: 将建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间，分别用堆叠的特征空间交互模块和任务空间交互模块建模；引入语义分词方案实现用户-物品解耦以加速推理；采用多序列交叉注意力分别捕获异质行为模式并结合自注意力增强交互建模；引入多视角FFN支持跨组件的灵活可扩展参数扩展。

Result: 在快手和快手极速版两个生产场景的在线A/B测试中，App停留时间分别提升+0.101%/+0.260%，观看时长分别提升+0.729%/+1.113%。

Conclusion: UniFormer通过统一的模型中心扩展框架，在效率和效果上均优于现有方法，验证了跨特征空间和任务空间的联合扩展在工业推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, substantial progress has been made in industrial recommendation through component-centric model scaling, where individual components such as behavior modeling, feature interaction, or task modeling are independently scaled to improve model capacity. Although recent methods such as HyFormer and OneTrans further explore cross-module co-scaling by jointly modeling behavior and interaction, their designs are still confined to the feature space and lack a unified model-centric scaling framework over the overall modeling space. In this paper, we propose UniFormer, an efficient and unified model-centric scaling framework for industrial recommender systems. To improve efficiency, UniFormer decomposes the overall modeling space into feature and task spaces, which are modeled by stacked Feature-space Interaction Modules and Task-space Interaction Modules, respectively. Moreover, UniFormer introduces semantic-based tokenization scheme to enable user-item decoupling, thereby achieving request-level inference acceleration. To prevent preference collapse, UniFormer employs multi-sequence cross-attention to separately capture heterogeneous behavior patterns, followed by the self-attention to enhance interaction modeling. Besides, dedicated multi-view FFNs are introduced to support flexible and scalable parameter scaling across different modeling components. Extensive online A/B testing in two production scenarios, Kuaishou and Kuaishou Lite, shows that UniFormer consistently improves user engagement and interaction metrics, achieving gains of +0.101%/+0.260% in App Stay Time and +0.729%/+1.113% in Watch Time, respectively.

</details>


### [105] [TRUST: Item-Calibrated Interval Evidence for Temporal Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27214)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Yifan Yin,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRUST是一个针对会话推荐的框架，它提出对时间间隔进行"物品相对化"校准——每个物品有自己的时间间隔分布，相同间隔对不同物品含义不同。通过基于经验分布的评分函数，TRUST在邻居采样、会话图编码和兴趣聚合三个模块中进行时间信号校准，从而提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时间的会话推荐方法默认使用绝对时间间隔值，隐含假设相同间隔在不同物品上携带相似的兴趣信号。作者通过实验发现该假设不成立：每个物品有其自身的时间间隔分布，同一间隔对不同物品的意义不同。

Method: 提出TRUST框架，核心是设计一个评分函数，将观测到的间隔相对于该物品对应的经验间隔分布进行评估。该评分函数用于指导三个环节：全局邻居采样、会话图编码和最终兴趣聚合。评分函数可作为一个模型无关的方法插入到现有时间会话推荐器中。

Result: 在公开数据集上，TRUST一致性地优于代表性的基于时间和非时间的基线方法。插拔实验表明评分函数能以模型无关方式提升现有时间会话推荐器。组件消融实验表明在每个模块内校准时间信号（而非移除模块本身）能持续改进邻居采样、会话图编码和兴趣聚合。

Conclusion: 时间间隔的意义应相对于物品自身分布来解读，TRUST通过物品级时间校准有效提升了会话推荐的性能，且其评分函数具有良好的通用性和可插拔性。

Abstract: Temporal signals have been widely used in session-based recommendation to infer user interest. Existing temporal session-based recommenders primarily rely on absolute interval values, implicitly assuming that the same interval carries similar interest signals across items. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not hold: each item has its own interval distribution, so an interval should be interpreted relative to the item it belongs to. Based on this observation, we propose TRUST, a framework that evaluates each observed interval relative to the empirical interval distribution of the corresponding item. Specifically, we propose a score function to guide global neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and final interest aggregation. Experiments on public datasets show that TRUST consistently improves over representative temporal and non-temporal baselines, and plug-in experiments further show that the proposed scoring function can improve existing temporal session recommenders as a model-agnostic method. Component-wise ablations further show that calibrating the temporal signals within each module, rather than removing the module itself, consistently improves neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and interest aggregation.

</details>


### [106] [NOVA: A Verification-Aware Agent Harness for Architecture Evolution in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27243)
*Shaohua Liu,Liang Fang,Yilong Sun,Shudong Huang,Qingsong Luo,Xiaoyang Chen,Dongqiang Liu,Chuangang Ma,Zhenzhen Chai,Henghuan Wang,Shijie Quan,Changyuan Cui,Zhangbin Zhu,Peng Chen,Wei Xu,Lei Xiao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NOVA是一个基于层级感知的智能体框架，通过架构梯度、验证级联和L1-L4任务控制，实现工业推荐系统中验证感知的架构演化，在L2和L3任务上分别达到54.5%和60.0%的有效通过率，将文献到生产的周期缩短13倍以上，并在在线A/B测试中实现pCVR GMV增长1.25%-2.02%同时降低偏差37.3%-66.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型通过架构演化持续改进，但开发此类升级依赖专家经验且难以规模化。现有方法如AutoML主要调超参数、通用LLM编码智能体优化可运行代码而非有效架构，导致静默失败。亟需一种能够验证感知的自动化架构演化方法。

Method: 提出NOVA框架：1）架构梯度——受SGD启发的非可微更新信号，聚合先验修改、验证诊断、指标反馈和轨迹记忆；2）验证级联——依次检查结构语义、本地可执行性、离线有效性和在线影响，早期阻断无效候选并记录失败模式为禁止方向；3）L1-L4层级任务控制——根据任务复杂度和风险匹配自动化程度，高风险任务交由Copilot人工监督。

Result: 在工业广告系统中，NOVA在L2 ScaleUp和L3 Literature-to-Production任务上分别达到54.5%和60.0%的最高有效通过率；相比编码智能体基线减少静默失败；文献到生产周期中人工参与时间缩短13倍以上；在线A/B测试中，选中的L3候选在三个pCVR目标上分别提升GMV +1.25%、+1.70%、+2.02%，同时降低pCVR偏差58.8%、66.7%和37.3%。

Conclusion: NOVA通过验证感知的架构演化方法，有效解决了工业推荐系统架构升级中自动化不足和静默失败的问题，在通过率、效率和经济指标上均取得显著提升，验证了层级感知控制与验证级联在架构演化中的有效性。

Abstract: Industrial advertising recommender models are continuously improved through architecture evolution. Upgrades such as RankMixer, TokenMixer-Large, and MixFormer show that better structures remain a key source of quality and business gains. Yet developing such upgrades in production is expert-intensive and difficult to scale. Existing automation is insufficient: AutoML mainly tunes hyper-parameters, while effective gains often require cross-module changes under strict constraints; generic LLM coding agents optimize for runnable code, but runnable code does not imply a valid recommender architecture. Candidates may pass local tests while causing silent failures that degrade performance.
  We present NOVA, a level-aware agent harness for verification-aware architecture evolution. NOVA uses an architecture gradient, an SGD-inspired, non-differentiable update signal that aggregates prior modifications, verification diagnostics, metric feedback, and trajectory memory to guide the next modification. A verification cascade checks structure semantics, local executability, offline effectiveness, and online impact; invalid candidates are blocked early, with failure patterns recorded as forbidden directions. L1--L4 task-level control matches automation to task complexity and risk, routing high-risk tasks to Copilot for human oversight. Deployed in an industrial advertising system, NOVA achieves the highest effective pass rate on L2 ScaleUp and L3 Literature-to-Production tasks (54.5% and 60.0%), reduces silent failures compared with coding-agent baselines, and shortens one literature-to-production cycle by over 13x in human-attended time. In online A/B testing, the selected L3 candidate improves GMV on three pCVR objectives by +1.25%, +1.70%, and +2.02%, while reducing pCVR bias by 58.8%, 66.7%, and 37.3%.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Orchestrating Black-Box Schema Converters: An Empirical Study of Automated, Quality-Ranked Conversion Across Heterogeneous Schema Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26180)
*Felix Neubauer,Giridhar Chinnikkaramadom Govindan,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过编排异构、有损的转换器，实现模式语言间的自动化、可重现、质量可排名的转换，并基于60个真实任务评估发现43个可成功转换，其余失败揭示了当前转换器生态的具体局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常需要在多种模式语言（如JSON Schema、XSD、SHACL）中维护同一数据模型，而现有转换器难以寻找、质量参差不齐且常有信息丢失，导致模型演化的维护负担沉重。
