Method: 将模式语言建模为节点、转换器建模为有向边，把转换视为路径搜索问题；对转换器作黑盒处理，通过发现、执行、排序路径并记录完整步骤来源，失败时尝试备选路径；实现为开源Schema Conversion Orchestrator，集成到MetaConfigurator中，使用智能体辅助+人工标注进行质量评估。

Result: 在60个跨5种模式语言的真实转换任务中，编排方法在43个任务上找到了可用结果，其余17个任务的失败精准定位了当前转换器生态的具体空白。

Conclusion: 编排方法能有效提升模式转换的自动化和可复现性，但仍存在转换器覆盖不足的问题；对工具构建者以及转换质量度量有启示意义。

Abstract: Modern software systems routinely need the same data model in several schema languages: a model may exist as JSON Schema for a web API, as XSD for data exchange, and as SHACL for a knowledge graph. Keeping these representations consistent as the model evolves is a recurring construction and maintenance burden, because converters between schema languages are hard to find, scattered across ecosystems, of uneven quality, and frequently lossy. We study, empirically, to what extent such imperfect, heterogeneous converters can be orchestrated into automated, reproducible, and quality-ranked conversions, and where the current converter landscape reaches its limits. Our approach models schema languages as nodes and converters, treated as black boxes, as directed edges, so that conversions become paths that are discovered, executed, ranked, and reported with full per-step provenance, with failures handled by trying alternatives. We realize it as the open-source Schema Conversion Orchestrator, integrate it into MetaConfigurator, and evaluate it on 60 conversion tasks built from real-world schemas across five schema languages, using agent-assisted, human-reviewed quality annotations. Orchestration surfaces a usable result for 43 of 60 tasks; the remaining failures localize concrete gaps in the converter landscape. We discuss implications for tool builders and for measuring conversion quality.

</details>


### [108] [Augmentation with Dilution: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Human Contributor Ecosystems After AI Coding Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26289)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究AI编码代理对开源项目中人类贡献者生态的影响，发现AI代理不会显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量，但会降低人类贡献者密度、减少新人的相对参与份额，并增加审查深度，呈现"增强伴随稀释"的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将人类贡献者视为静态背景，缺乏以人类贡献者生态系统为因变量的大规模实证研究，因此本文旨在探究AI编码代理采纳如何改变开源项目中人类参与者的数量、构成和行为。

Method: 采用交错双重差分（staggered difference-in-differences）设计，使用Sun and Abraham估计量，基于2023年1月至2026年5月间11,097个GitHub仓库的数据集进行因果推断。

Result: AI代理采纳不显著改变人类贡献者绝对数量（ATT=0.014, p=0.224），但显著降低人类贡献者密度（ATT=-0.019, p=0.002）；新人相对参与份额下降3.7个百分点（ATT=-0.037, p<0.001）；审查深度增加5.3%（ATT=+0.0168, p<0.001）；效应随项目规模、编程语言和项目成熟度系统变化。

Conclusion: AI代理并未取代人类贡献者，而是系统性地重塑了开源生态系统的参与结构，呈现"增强伴随稀释"的模式：AI将负担从代码生产阶段转移到审查阶段。

Abstract: AI coding agents are penetrating open-source software development at an unprecedented pace, yet existing research predominantly treats human contributors as a static backdrop rather than as the subject of inquiry. This paper presents the first large-scale empirical study that takes the human contributor ecosystem as its dependent variable, examining how the number, composition, and behavior of human participants change following AI coding agent adoption in open-source projects. Using a staggered difference-in-differences design on a dataset of 11,097 GitHub repositories spanning January 2023 to May 2026, we provide causal evidence via the Sun and Abraham estimator. Our results show that AI agent adoption does not significantly change the absolute number of human contributors (ATT = 0.014, p = 0.224), but significantly reduces human contributor density (ATT = -0.019, p = 0.002), indicating that the relative share of human participation declines as AI-generated pull requests accumulate. The relative participation share of newcomers declines significantly by 3.7 percentage points (ATT = -0.037, p < 0.001), with the effect emerging immediately after adoption and remaining stable throughout the observation window. Review depth increases significantly by 5.3% (ATT = +0.0168, p < 0.001), indicating that AI agents shift burden from the code production stage to the review stage. Moderator analysis reveals that these effects vary systematically with project size, programming language, and project maturity. Together, these findings present a pattern of augmentation with dilution: AI agents are not displacing human contributors, but are systematically reshaping the participation structure of open-source ecosystems.

</details>


### [109] [Towards Safety-Aware Mutation Testing for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26456)
*Donghwan Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶系统的安全感知变异测试方法（SAMT），通过在模块间消息中注入时间受限的故障来模拟实际交互失败，并基于STPA等安全工程框架衍生变异生成规则，以提供更有效的测试充分性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统测试缺乏何时停止生成测试场景的系统性准则，已有的覆盖度指标要么关注单一组件可靠性，要么将ADS视为黑盒，无法捕捉导致大多数事故的组件交互问题；传统变异测试直接移植代码级或深度学习模型级变异到ADS各模块也不够充分。

Method: 提出安全感知变异测试（SAMT），核心创新包括：(1) 在ADS模块间交换的消息中注入时间受限的故障，而非在单个组件内部注入人工故障；(2) 从自上而下的安全工程框架（如STPA）推导变异生成规则，确保变异体代表真实危害。

Result: 该方法有望提供严格的测试充分性评估机制，支持自动化场景生成，并指导ADS修复。论文同时指出了关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: SAMT通过将系统思维嵌入变异测试流程，为ADS测试充分性评估提供了范式转变，但相关技术路径仍需进一步探索和验证。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is essential for ensuring the safety of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS), yet the community lacks a systematic criterion for determining when we can safely stop additional test scenario generation. Existing coverage metrics typically focus on individual component reliability or treat the ADS as a black box, failing to capture certain component interactions that cause most ADS accidents. While traditional mutation testing provides a falsifiable measure of test adequacy, directly porting code- and deep learning model-level mutations to the corresponding modules of ADS is insufficient.
  In this vision paper, we propose a paradigm shift toward Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT). Unlike traditional mutation testing, which creates mutants (i.e., faulty versions of the software under test) by injecting artificial faults into individual components, SAMT systematically injects temporally bounded faults into the messages exchanged between ADS modules to simulate realistic interaction failures. To ensure these mutants represent genuine hazards, we propose deriving mutant generation rules directly from top-down safety engineering frameworks, such as System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). By embedding systems thinking into the mutation testing pipeline, SAMT provides a rigorous mechanism for evaluating test adequacy, enabling automated scenario generation, and guiding ADS repair. We also outline critical open challenges.

</details>


### [110] [An Empirical Study of LLM-Generated Specifications for VeriFast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26490)
*Wen Fan,Minh Tran,Sanya Dod,Xin Hu,Marilyn Rego,Danning Xie,Jenna DiVincenzo,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLMs在生成C函数（303个）的分离逻辑验证器VeriFast规范时的表现，发现功能保持率超91%，但验证成功率仅31.4%，94%的错误源于LLMs对SL验证器领域知识的欠缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离逻辑验证器虽能验证工业级软件，但需要大量人工编写辅助规范（specifications）。现有研究将LLMs用于生成代码、测试和证明，但主要针对非SL验证器。因此需要填补LLMs在SL验证器规范生成方面的空白。

Method: 以VeriFast为SL验证器，303个C函数为基准，采用8种提示方法、10种LLM和3种输入类型，分两阶段进行实验，通过定量和定性分析评估LLM生成的代码和规范的功能正确性、可验证性和错误类型。

Result: 源代码和规范的功能行为保持率均超91%，但验证成功率仅31.4%。Gemini 2.5 Pro和提供形式化契约能提高成功率。94%的错误来自LLMs对SL验证器（如VeriFast）的领域知识理解错误。

Conclusion: LLMs在生成SL验证器规范时功能保持较好但验证成功率低，主要瓶颈是领域知识缺失。研究结果为优化LLM生成的SL验证器规范提供了指导。

Abstract: Static verification tools can assure industrial scale software, but require significant human labor to write specifications. This is particularly true of static verifiers based on separation logic (SL verifiers), which excel at verifying heapmanipulating programs, but require many complex auxiliary specifications to reason about heap structure. Recent work applies large language models (LLMs) to generate code, tests, and proofs, including specifications for verifiers, but mostly targeting non-SL verifiers. To address this gap, this paper thoroughly evaluates how well LLMs perform when prompted to generate specifications for verifying 303 C functions with the SL verifier VeriFast. We explored eight prompting approaches, ten LLMs, and three input types in two stages. Quantitative and qualitative analyses are used to assess the LLM-generated code and specifications for functional behavior, verifiability and errors. The results show that LLMs preserve functional behavior in source code and specifications (both over 91%), but achieve modest verification success (31.4%). Using Gemini 2.5 Pro and providing formal contracts lead to higher success rates in our setting. Moreover, most errors (94%) come from LLMs' mistakes in the domainspecific knowledge of SL verifiers such as VeriFast. These findings provide guidance for optimizing LLM-generated specifications for SL verifiers.

</details>


### [111] [Evaluation-Strategy Gap in Fault Diagnosis of Deep Learning Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26492)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 深度学习程序训练失败诊断技术存在性能差距：在跨程序部署场景下，现有技术的平衡准确率比程序内验证低0.190，主要原因是程序级特征结构差异。曲率特征对未见程序有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对已见程序有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习程序训练失败诊断技术的评估通常采用程序内交叉验证，但这种方法可能无法反映在未见过的程序上的实际部署性能。需要量化这种性能差距并识别其根本原因。

Method: 使用DynFault语料库，包含来自38个真实世界深度学习程序的5,542个故障注入训练轨迹。比较程序内评估与留出整个程序的评估之间的平衡准确率差距，并分析曲率特征、优化器特征和激活特征在不同评估设置下的行为差异。

Result: 程序内评估与留出程序评估之间平衡准确率差距为0.190。差距源于程序级特征结构。曲率特征对未见程序的失稳检测有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对已见程序有帮助。

Conclusion: 现有故障诊断技术在跨程序部署场景下性能显著下降，特征选择对泛化能力至关重要：曲率特征更具跨程序鲁棒性，而优化器和激活特征存在过拟合风险。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) programs can fail during training for many reasons, and diagnosing the cause is a costly and time-consuming maintenance task. Techniques for diagnosing such failures are commonly assessed using within-program cross-validation, which may be inadequate for deployment settings involving previously unseen programs. It is therefore necessary to assess how performance differs across these settings and to identify the causes of any performance gap in established fault diagnosis techniques for DL. We investigate this gap using DynFault, a corpus of 5,542 fault-injected training traces from 38 real-world DL programs. We found a gap of 0.190 in balanced accuracy for existing fault diagnosis techniques between within-program evaluation and holding out whole programs. We also found the gap comes from program-level structure in the features, which led us to examine two runtime feature sets, curvature features and optimizer features, and their behavior on unseen programs. We found that curvature features are useful for instability detection on unseen programs, while optimizer and activation features help only on programs seen during training.

</details>


### [112] [Same Scrutiny, More Time: Eye Tracking Insights into Reviewing LLM-Labelled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26505)
*Ranim Khojah,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Mazen Mohamad,Julian Frattini,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验发现，虽然明确标注为LLM生成的代码标签并未改变开发者的审查彻底性，但显著增加了他们对代码的注视时长，且开发者会根据逻辑正确性等特定标准调整审查策略，揭示了审查意图与实际行为之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码存在错误和幻觉风险，需要仔细审查，但开发者对LLM生成代码的信任程度和实际审查行为尚不明确，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz实验设计，通过眼动追踪和行为数据收集，结合贝叶斯数据分析和定性分析，研究开发者在代码审查任务中面对明确标注为LLM生成的代码时的行为变化。

Result: LLM标签未改变审查彻底性，但增加了对标注代码的注视时长；开发者会根据逻辑正确性等标准调整审查策略，并使用提示信息指导审查。审查意图与实际行为存在差距。

Conclusion: LLM标签会影响开发者的注意力分配和审查策略，但未必提高审查彻底性；建议将提示作为软件工件纳入LLM工具设计，并呼吁软件公司重新审视AI政策以更好地支持开发者审查LLM生成代码。

Abstract: Modern software development increasingly involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate code. Despite their rapid advancement, LLMs remain prone to errors and hallucinations, emphasizing the importance of careful code inspection. However, in practice, developers' trust in LLM-generated code and their willingness to review it thoroughly may differ from these recommendations. How developers actually behave when reviewing LLM-generated code remains largely unexplored. In this study, we conduct a Wizard-of-Oz experiment to examine how software engineers behave when code is explicitly labeled as LLM-generated during a code review task. We collect both behavioral data and participant feedback through eye-tracking and exit interviews. Combining Bayesian data analysis with qualitative analysis, we found that while the thoroughness of code review did not change for participants, they spent more time fixating on LLM-labelled code, indicating that the label itself influences attention. Practitioners also adapted their review strategy for LLM-labelled code by assessing the code based on specific criteria (e.g., logical correctness), or using the prompt to guide their review. These findings inform LLM-based tool design on labelling while incorporating the prompt as a software artifact. Our study reveals a gap between reviewers' intentions and actual reviewing behaviour, highlighting the need for software companies to revisit their AI policies (particularly regarding LLM-assisted development) to better support developers in reviewing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [113] [ConcoLixir: Reactive LLM Discovery Oracles for Python Concolic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26545)
*Dong Chen,Chih-Duo Hong,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConcoLixir将LLM作为发现预言机集成到Python的符号执行框架中，在求解器失败时提供种子和输入建议，在覆盖率停滞时定位未覆盖代码。在合成、真实世界和库目标上，相较于无LLM的基线，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点，总API成本仅$1.63。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python程序的混合执行（concolic testing）面临库调用导致符号变量降级、正则/校验和/解析器等语义操作难以求解、已有路径覆盖停滞等反复出现的限制。

Method: 提出ConcoLixir，一种反应式LLM扩展：LLM作为发现预言机而非求解器或正确性预言机的替代。三个核心作用：生成初始种子、在求解器失败后提出具体输入、在覆盖率停滞时定位未覆盖代码。每个候选输入均通过混合执行执行，仅用观测到的覆盖率和收集的路径约束指导后续探索。

Result: 在合成、真实世界和库目标上，ConcoLixir相较于无LLM预言机的基线混合执行测试器，平均行覆盖率分别提升8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点。增益在语义障碍和库边界附近最为显著，完整评估的API费用仅$1.63。

Conclusion: 有界的LLM发现可以补充符号推理，而无需替代它。

Abstract: Concolic testing combines concrete execution with symbolic constraint solving, but Python programs expose recurring limits. Library calls can cause symbolic variables to downgrade to concrete values. Regular expressions, checksums, parsers, and other semantic operations can be hard to solve, and exploration can plateau on already covered paths. We present ConcoLixir, a reactive LLM extension for Python concolic execution. The LLM acts as a discovery oracle, not a replacement for the solver or a correctness oracle. It generates initial seeds, proposes concrete inputs after solver failures, and targets uncovered code when coverage stalls. Each candidate is executed concolically, and only observed coverage and collected path constraints guide later exploration. Across synthetic, real-world, and library targets, ConcoLixir improves mean line coverage over the baseline concolic tester without an LLM oracle by 8.6, 15.1, and 17.0 percentage points. The gains are strongest near semantic barriers and library boundaries, and the full evaluation costs \$1.63 in API charges. These results show that bounded LLM discovery can complement symbolic reasoning without replacing it.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Mutant Equivalence via Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26604)
*José Campos,Andriy Miranskyy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于转译器（Transpiler-Based Equivalence, TBE）的轻量级方法，用于识别量子软件变异测试中的等价变异体。该方法通过将原始电路和变异电路在相同配置下转译并比较其OpenQASM代码来判定等价性。在348,299个存活变异体（其中92,011个为等价变异体）上评估，TBE识别出29,536个（32.1%），实现了100%的精确率和82%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件变异测试中存在未解决的等价变异体问题——即语法不同但语义相同的变异体无法被任何测试杀死，会浪费测试工作并扭曲变异得分。先前研究生成超70万量子电路变异体，约半数存活，但无法判断是测试不足还是语义等价所致。

Method: 提出Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE)方法：将原始电路和变异电路在相同配置下进行转译，然后比较两者生成的OpenQASM代码，若代码相同则判定为等价变异体。

Result: 在348,299个存活变异体（其中92,011个为等价变异体）上评估，TBE识别出29,536个等价变异体（32.1%），精确率100%，准确率82%。

Conclusion: TBE是一种轻量级且高精度的量子变异等价性检测方法，能有效识别量子电路中的等价变异体，解决量子变异测试中的关键问题。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test suite quality by introducing artificial faults (mutants) and checking whether tests detect (kill) them. A central challenge is the equivalent mutant problem: some mutants are syntactically different from the original program but semantically identical to it and therefore cannot be killed by any test. If left unidentified, such mutants waste testing effort and distort mutation scores. In quantum software, mutation testing is increasingly used, but the equivalent mutant problem remains unsolved. A recent study generated more than 700,000 quantum circuit mutants and found that roughly half survived the available tests, making it unclear whether these survivors reflect weak tests or semantic equivalence. We propose Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE), a lightweight approach that identifies equivalent quantum mutants by transpiling original and mutated circuits under the same configuration and comparing their resulting OpenQASM code. We evaluate TBE on 348,299 surviving mutants, 92,011 of which are equivalent; TBE identifies 29,536 of them (32.1%) as equivalent while achieving 100% precision and 82% accuracy.

</details>


### [115] [Knowledge-Based Pull Requests: A Trusted Workflow for Agent-Mediated Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26721)
*Xinyu Zhang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出知识型拉取请求（KPR）工作流，将外部协作者的代码、测试和代理交互轨迹视为知识源而非默认合并候选，通过项目内可信代理在本地环境中重新生成代码，分离了“知识是否应进入项目”与“特定实现是否应合并”两个决策，并通过7个真实PR的模拟实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PR将外部贡献的代码直接作为合并候选，导致理解意图、协商范围和管理长期项目责任的高成本。AI编码代理使得代码生成变得廉价，但信任和协作治理问题仍未解决，需要一种跨信任边界的可信协作工作流。

Method: 提出KPR工作流：①外部协作者提交本地代码、测试和清洗后的代理交互轨迹作为知识源；②代理将知识源提炼为人类确认的知识包（设计备忘录、风险清单、测试计划、实现简报）；③项目内的可信编码代理在接收项目的环境（仓库上下文、工程规范、测试和安全策略）中重新生成候选代码。贡献包括：KPR工作流、候选工件模式、成本核算视图、协作网关架构、7个公开PR的受控模拟实验。

Result: 模拟实验表明，KPR包可从真实PR材料实例化，并在描述消融、差异消融和合成毒化补丁条件下经受压力测试。

Conclusion: KPR是一个可经验测试的工作流，其价值取决于可审计的提取、转换和项目端再生是否能降低理解和重构高上下文外部变更的成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents are changing the bottleneck in software collaboration: code is increasingly cheap, while understanding intent, negotiating scope, and governing long-term project responsibility remain costly. This paper proposes \emph{Knowledge-Based Pull Requests} (KPR), a trusted workflow for agent-mediated software collaboration across trust boundaries, including open source, enterprise, vendor, contractor, and customer-driven settings. In KPR, an external collaborator's local code, tests, and cleaned agent interaction trace are treated as knowledge sources rather than as the default merge candidate. Agents distill these sources into a human-confirmed knowledge package and render it into reviewer-facing forms such as design memos, risk checklists, test plans, or implementation briefs. A project-owned inner trusted coding agent then regenerates candidate code inside the receiving project's environment under repository context, engineering conventions, tests, and security policy. KPR therefore separates two decisions that traditional pull requests often collapse: whether the knowledge should enter the project, and whether a particular implementation should be merged. We contribute the KPR workflow, a candidate artifact schema, a cost-accounting view, a collaboration gateway architecture, a minimal controlled simulation pilot over seven merged public pull requests, and an evaluation agenda. The pilot shows that KPR packages can be instantiated from real PR material and stress-tested under description ablation, diff ablation, and synthetic poisoned-patch conditions. We position KPR as an empirically testable workflow: its value depends on whether auditable extraction, transformation, and project-side regeneration reduce the cost of understanding and reworking high-context external changes.

</details>


### [116] [A Deterministic Control Plane for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26924)
*Padmaraj Madatha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对LLM编码工具中未受管理的代理配置文件层，提出确定性控制平面Rel(AI)Build，通过内容寻址、权限管理、状态机等方法实现配置治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码工具赋予代理广泛的文件和shell访问权限，但配置层（规则文件、代理定义、IDE特定markdown）缺乏管理，存在安全风险和治理空白。

Method: 对10,008个GitHub仓库进行流行度研究（n=6,145个代理配置文件），发现配置传播模式；提出Rel(AI)Build：SHA-256内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、层级权限、攻击衍生阻止列表、阶段状态机、多IDE编译、Jaccard相似度检测提示漂移。

Result: 10.1%的追踪路径在不同仓库间是SHA-256精确重复（fork调整、阈值独立），75.5%的克隆对跨组织边界；58%为单次提交配置，age-normalized提交率低于CI/CD工作流；<1%的代理配置声明权限边界（vs Actions工作流33%）。一致性测试验证各机制执行其声明的约束。

Conclusion: LLM代理配置层需要确定性、工具无关的治理，不应委托给进一步的LLM编排。

Abstract: LLM coding harnesses grant agents broad file and shell access, yet the configuration layer that steers them -- rules files, agent definitions, IDE-specific markdown -- is largely unmanaged. A prevalence study of 10,008 public GitHub repositories (n=6,145 agent config files) finds that agent configurations propagate as undeclared shared components: 10.1% of tracked paths are SHA-256 exact duplicates across independent repositories (fork-adjusted, threshold-independent), with 75.5% of clone pairs crossing organisational boundaries. Two further patterns are indicative: configurations are rarely revised (58% single-commit; 0.4 vs 0.6 commits/month age-normalised against CI/CD workflows), and rarely declare permission boundaries (<1% of agent configs vs 33% of Actions workflows, n=31 true positives).
  We propose a deterministic control plane above the harness that maps one-to-one to these gaps. Rel(AI)Build treats agent definitions as a managed supply chain (SHA-256 content addressing, HMAC-stamped lockfiles, hash-chained audit logs); enforces tiered permissions and attack-derived blocklists before LLM invocation; gates feature work through a phase state machine with requirement-to-file-to-test traceability; compiles a single canonical definition to seven IDE targets; and detects prompt drift via Jaccard similarity. Conformance tests on injected violations confirm each mechanism enforces its stated invariant; developer outcomes remain future work. Governance of this layer must be deterministic and tool-agnostic -- not delegated to further LLM orchestration.

</details>


### [117] [Are LLMs Ready for Anti-Pattern Detection in Microservice Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26927)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Amalfitano,Porfirio Tramontana,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否通过基于提示的分析流程检测微服务架构中的架构反模式，并与静态分析工具MARS进行对比。结果表明LLMs在局部、异构或语义丰富的反模式检测上具有竞争力，但在需要显式结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上表现受限，可作为传统静态分析的补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统容易反复出现架构反模式，影响可维护性、可演化性和运行质量。现有检测方法依赖静态分析和手工规则，存在工具依赖性强、检测逻辑固定、难以适应异构代码库等问题，因此需要探索更灵活、适应性更强的检测方法。

Method: 构建了一个带有16种架构反模式标注的微服务仓库基准数据集，设计了基于提示的分析流程，评估了三种通用LLMs在反模式检测上的表现，并与最先进的静态分析工具MARS在精度和召回率上进行了统一对比。

Result: LLMs在局部性较强、异构性高或语义丰富的反模式上取得了与MARS相当甚至更优的性能；但在需要显式结构信息或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上，LLMs表现明显不如静态分析方法。

Conclusion: LLMs尚不能替代传统静态分析工具，但已可作为微服务架构评估中一个有前景的互补辅助手段。

Abstract: Microservice systems are prone to recurrent architectural anti-patterns (APs) that hinder maintainability, evolvability, and operational quality. Most existing AP detection approaches rely on static analysis and handcrafted rules, which can be effective but are often tool-dependent, limited to explicitly encoded detection logic, and difficult to adapt to heterogeneous repositories. In this paper, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) are ready to support architectural anti-pattern detection in microservice architectures through a prompt-based analysis pipeline over static repository artifacts. We evaluate three general-purpose LLMs on a curated benchmark of microservice repositories annotated with 16 architectural anti-patterns, and compare their performance against the state-of-the-art static-analysis tool MARS using a uniform evaluation protocol based on precision and recall. Our results show that LLMs can provide useful support for anti-pattern detection, achieving competitive performance on several anti-patterns, especially when the relevant evidence is local, heterogeneous, or semantically rich. At the same time, they exhibit clear limitations on anti-patterns that require explicit structural or cross-service dependency evidence, where static analysis remains more reliable. These findings suggest that LLMs are not yet a replacement for traditional analyzers, but already represent a promising complementary aid for architectural assessment in microservice systems.

</details>


### [118] [CLIR: Liveness-Driven and Structure-Aware Fuzzing for the Cranelift Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26977)
*Shangtong Cao,Tianlei Song,Qiuping Yi,Tianyu Chen,Guoai Xu,Ningyu He,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CLIR是一个针对Cranelift编译器的差分测试框架，通过语法保持的分层生成策略、活性引导的指令细化机制和诊断引导的跨架构适应方案，分别解决了SSA形式有效性、计算密度和跨架构根因分析三大挑战。实验表明，CLIR检测到的独特bug数量是现有基准方法的8倍至24倍，并在72小时内发现24个跨架构bug，其中21个已确认、9个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cranelift作为WebAssembly和Rust（实验性）的快速编译器后端，日益重要，但现有编译器测试方法难以应对其三大独特挑战：SSA形式下有效IR的构造、高计算密度指令序列的生成，以及异构架构后端的高效根因分析。

Method: CLIR框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 语法保持的分层生成策略，保证SSA合法性；(2) 活性引导的指令细化机制，最大化计算密度；(3) 诊断引导的跨架构适应方案，促进异构后端的高效根因分析。

Result: 相比cranelift-fuzzgen、wasm-smith、WASMaker和RustSmith四种基线，CLIR分别检测到8倍、24倍、8倍和无穷倍（RustSmith发现0个bug）的独特bug数量。在72小时测试内发现24个跨所有目标架构的bug，21个被确认，9个已修复。

Conclusion: CLIR框架有效解决了Cranelift编译器测试中的关键挑战，显著优于现有方法，能够高效发现跨架构的编译器缺陷。

Abstract: Modern compilers are complex software systems that must correctly translate high-level programming languages into machine code across multiple architectures. Cranelift, a fast and modern compiler backend originally developed for WebAssembly and recently adopted as an experimental backend for Rust, has gained increasing importance due to its superior compilation speed compared to LLVM and comprehensive multi-architecture support, including x86-64, AArch64, s390x, and RISCV64. However, despite decades of development in compiler testing, testing Cranelift still presents unique challenges, including (1) constructing valid IR under the strict enforcement of SSA form, (2) generating sequences with sufficient computational density to stress backend components, and (3) balancing broad backend coverage with efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous architectures.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLIR, a differential testing framework that integrates a syntax-preserving hierarchical generation strategy to guarantee SSA validity, a liveness-guided instruction refinement mechanism to maximize computational density, and a diagnosis-guided cross-architecture adaptation scheme to facilitate efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous backends. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that CLIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, detecting 8x, 24x, and 8x more unique bugs than cranelift-fuzzgen, wasm-smith, and WASMaker, respectively, while RustSmith uncovered no bugs. Consequently, within 72 hours of testing, CLIR discovered 24 bugs spanning all target architectures, with 21 confirmed and 9 fixed.

</details>


### [119] [To Run or Not to Run: Analyzing the Cost-Effectiveness of Code Execution in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26978)
*Zhihao Lin,Junhua Zhu,Mingyi Zhou,Xin Wang,Zhensu Sun,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究发现，在LLM程序修复代理中，代码执行虽被广泛使用（平均每任务8.8次），但限制执行对修复成功率影响极小（商业代理上仅差1.25个百分点且不显著），同时可大幅节约token和运行时间成本。执行带来的收益集中在少数实例上，表明当前代理不加区分地使用执行，应将其视为有明确成本效益权衡的资源而非默认能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行操作在LLM程序修复代理中被广泛使用且耗时昂贵，但其实际影响尚未被充分探索。作者希望系统性地量化执行行为的成本效益，以指导更高效的修复策略设计。

Method: 两阶段实证研究：第一阶段分析SWE-bench排行榜上7,745条代理轨迹的执行行为；第二阶段在200个SWE-bench实例上评估三个代理（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）在四种执行范式下的3,000次端到端修复尝试，比较性能与成本。

Result: (1) 所有代理和模型都使用执行，平均每任务8.8次，执行频率在2-19次间变化，后期执行成功率更高；(2) 限制执行对修复成功率影响很小，商业代理上Prohibited与Unrestricted差距仅1.25个百分点且不显著，但能节省大量token和运行时间；(3) 执行收益集中在少数实例上而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 当前代理不加区分地使用执行，为收益甚微的实例支付了成本。执行应被视为一个有明确成本效益权衡的资源，而非默认能力，需要更精准地决定何时执行。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for program repair are increasingly built on a "generate-run-revise" paradigm, iteratively executing tests to evaluate and refine patches. This execution-based approach has become standard practice in state-of-the-art systems. However, executions can be time-consuming and expensive, yet their impact on these agents remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a two-stage empirical study over execution behavior in LLM-based program repair. To characterize execution behavior at scale, we first analyze 7,745 agent traces from SWE-bench leaderboard submissions. Second, we evaluate 3,000 end-to-end repair attempts across 200 SWE-bench instances and three agents (Claude Code, Codex, and the open-source OpenCode) under four execution paradigms, which allows for a fine-grained comparison of performance and cost. Our analysis reveals three key observations: (1) Code execution is used across all agents and models analyzed, with an average of 8.8 test runs per task. Execution behavior varies substantially across agents and models, with frequency ranging from 2 to 19 per task, and late-stage executions consistently achieve higher success rates than early-stage ones. (2) Execution restrictions have little effect on repair success: on commercial agents with SOTA models the resolve-rate gap between Prohibited and Unrestricted is only 1.25 percentage points and not statistically significant, while Prohibited saves substantial token and wall-clock cost. (3) Execution benefit is concentrated rather than uniform. These patterns suggest that current agents apply execution indiscriminately, paying its cost on instances where it provides little benefit. Execution, therefore, should be treated as a resource with an explicit cost-benefit tradeoff, not a default capability.

</details>


### [120] [How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26979)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Yizhuo Yang,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过轻量级静态分析（如调用图、继承层次等结构关系）为LLM代码代理提供确定性锚点，以替代纯关键词搜索。实验表明，将结构信息以纯文本注释形式注入，能使代码定位更精准（Func@5提升2.2个百分点）、轨迹更短（减少1.6轮交互），并显著降低运行间方差（链接跟随率从0.15-0.18提升至0.21-0.24），但最优粒度和方向性取决于仓库特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的代码代理主要依赖关键词搜索进行仓库导航，缺乏对调用图、继承层次、配置依赖等结构关系的感知，导致导航行为随机性强、难以复现。作者希望探究轻量级静态分析能否为这些代理提供确定性锚点，使导航更可预测和可复现。

Method: 以OpenAI的Codex为强基线，系统性地注入不同粒度和方向性的结构注释（纯文本形式），测量其对代码定位、轨迹行为和运行间稳定性的影响。设计了三种粒度：轻量级拓扑（调用/继承关系）、方向性控制（正向/反向）和密集标签。

Result: (1) 调用/继承拓扑使函数定位Func@5提升2.2个百分点，交互轮次减少1.6轮；(2) 对于枢纽密集项目，仅反向链接（"谁调用我"）效果最佳，正向边反而有害；(3) 标签将链接跟随率从0.15-0.18提升至0.21-0.24，运行间方差减半，Pass@1提升3.4个百分点，但输入token增加约10%。

Conclusion: 静态结构帮助代理的方式不是使其"更智能"，而是使其导航行为更规范、可复现，即"确定性锚定效应"。建议对中型项目默认使用轻量级拓扑，大型项目裁剪正向边，隐式依赖场景使用密集标签。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents navigate repositories through keyword search but miss the structural relationships, such as call graphs, inheritance hierarchies, and configuration dependencies, that define how software actually works. This makes agent navigation stochastic and difficult to reproduce across runs. We investigate whether lightweight static analysis can provide deterministic anchors for these agents: stable structural facts injected as plain-text comments that constrain probabilistic exploration and make navigation more predictable. Starting from a strong baseline, Codex from OpenAI, we systematically inject varying granularities of structural annotations and measure their effects on localization, trajectory behavior, and run-to-run stability. Our study identifies what we call the deterministic anchoring effect: static structure helps less by making agents "smarter" and more by making their navigation disciplined and reproducible. Three observations support this finding: (1) Anchoring works: lightweight call/inheritance topology improves function-level localization (+2.2pp Func@5) and shortens trajectories (-1.6 interaction rounds); (2) Anchoring is scale-sensitive: the optimal granularity and directionality depend on repository characteristics, where denser semantics show diminishing returns and hub-heavy projects benefit from inverse-only links that expose "who-calls-me" without forward edges; (3) Anchoring stabilizes: tags raise link-following rate from 0.15-0.18 to 0.21-0.24, roughly halve run-to-run variance, and improve single-run reliability (Pass@1 +3.4 pp) on medium-scale repositories, at the cost of roughly 10% more input tokens. These observations suggest practical guidelines: default to lightweight topology on medium projects, prune forward edges in large repositories, and reserve dense tags for implicit-dependency cases.

</details>


### [121] [Cleaning Logs for Downstream Tasks (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27000)
*Zahra G. Yazdi,Van-Hoang Le,Nyyti Saarimäki,Donghwan Shin,Domenico Bianculli,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志模板间依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法，旨在识别并移除与系统执行行为无关的自由消息，以提升下游分析任务（如模型推断和异常检测）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志中存在大量冗余和无关信息（自由消息），这些信息会负面影响下游分析任务（如模型推断和异常检测）的性能。

Method: 提出LogPurifier方法，基于日志消息模板之间的依赖关系，以任务无关的方式识别并移除自由消息，从而清洗日志数据。

Result: 计划通过受控实验设计，评估LogPurifier对两个下游任务（模型推断和异常检测）的有效性和效率的影响。

Conclusion: LogPurifier通过任务无关的日志清洗方式，有望提升下游分析任务的性能，但具体结论有待实验验证。

Abstract: Background: Software systems generate logs during execution to record critical events and runtime information for troubleshooting and monitoring. However, in practice, logs often contain significant amounts of redundant and irrelevant information, which can negatively impact the performance of downstream analysis tasks, such as model inference and anomaly detection. Objective: The objective of this study is to clean log data by identifying and removing free-standing messages -- messages that are not relevant to the execution behaviors of interest and are interleaved with messages capturing the system's functional behavior. Method: To address this objective, we propose LogPurifier, a task-agnostic log-cleaning approach based on dependency relationships between log message templates. The paper presents a plan for an empirical evaluation using a controlled experimental design to assess the impact of LogPurifier on the effectiveness and efficiency of two downstream tasks: model inference and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [122] [The Spec Growth Engine: Spec-Anchored, Code-Coupled, Drift-Enforced Architecture for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27045)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Spec Growth Engine轻量级框架，通过机器可读的规范图、Spine上下文组装器、垂直切片增长协议和漂移门限，解决AI编码代理在代码库规模扩展时出现的上下文爆炸和规范-代码静默漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理在加速开发的同时引入了两种结构失效模式：上下文爆炸（代理需推理整个代码库，上下文窗口填满后输出质量下降）和静默规范-代码漂移（代码演进但规范未更新，差异不可见直至修复成本高昂）。现有规范驱动方法未能完全解决这些问题。

Method: Spec Growth Engine包含四个核心组件：(1) 机器可读的规范图，节点携带显式的契约/设计分离；(2) Spine上下文组装器，将代理上下文限定在所有权路径上；(3) 垂直切片增长协议，强制执行最难优先的排序；(4) 漂移门限，将规范-代码偏离设为阻塞合并条件。该设计综合了Parnas信息隐藏、C4、ADR、Walking Skeleton、反射模型、适应度函数等成熟软件工程原则，形成轻量级、代码耦合、机器可强制执行的整体方案。

Result: 该框架在无RUP/MDA等重量级框架开销的前提下，实现了对两种失效模式的结构性解决。

Conclusion: 通过将成熟的软件工程原则系统性地整合为轻量级机器可执行框架，Spec Growth Engine能够有效约束AI编码代理的行为，防止代码库规模增长带来的质量退化。

Abstract: AI coding agents dramatically accelerate implementation speed but introduce two structural failure modes that existing spec-driven approaches do not fully solve: (1) context explosion -- the agent must reason over an entire repository at once, degrading output quality as the context window fills; and (2) silent spec-code drift -- code evolves, the specification does not, and the divergence becomes invisible until it is costly to repair.
  We present the Spec Growth Engine, a lightweight framework that addresses both failure modes through a machine-readable spec graph whose nodes carry explicit contract/design separation, a Spine context assembler that scopes agent context to an ownership path, a vertical-slice growth protocol that enforces hardest-first ordering, and a drift gate that makes spec-code divergence a blocking merge condition.
  The design synthesises well-established software engineering principles (Parnas information hiding, C4, ADRs, Walking Skeleton, Reflexion Models, Fitness Functions) into a lean, code-coupled, machine-enforced whole -- without the overhead of heavy-weight frameworks such as RUP or MDA.

</details>


### [123] [ATGBuilder: Feature-Assisted Graph Learning for Activity Transition Graph Construction with Seed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27080)
*Chenhui Cui,Zixiang Xian,Danyu Li,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ATGBuilder，一种基于特征辅助图学习的方法，用于种子监督的活动转换图构建。该方法利用大语言模型总结UI活动元数据，并显式建模widget触发信息作为边属性，通过辅助重构目标进行训练，显著优于现有方法，并能改进自动化GUI探索工具的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的活动转换图（ATG）构建方法存在局限：静态分析可能遗漏可行转换或提取不可行转换，动态探索则导致转换不完整。尽管基于学习的方法将ATG构建视为种子监督的链接预测任务，但对活动布局和widget触发信息的利用仍然有限。

Method: ATGBuilder采用大语言模型（LLM）从布局中总结UI活动元数据为紧凑的文本功能摘要；显式建模widget触发信息作为边属性，并在训练过程中使用辅助的widget属性重构目标来学习这些信息。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ATGBuilder显著优于现有最优方法。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。此外，通过更好的导航引导，ATGBuilder能够提升自动化GUI探索工具的性能。

Conclusion: ATGBuilder通过特征辅助的图学习方法，有效利用了活动布局和widget触发信息，在种子监督的ATG构建任务上取得了显著改进，并展示了其在实际GUI探索工具中的实用价值。

Abstract: Android applications are organized around activities that provide visual Graphical User Interface (GUI) containers that host the UI and handle user interaction events. Activity Transition Graphs (ATGs) have been widely used to model apps' GUI navigation. However, the construction of high-quality ATGs is challenging: ATGs based on static analysis may miss acceptable transitions and may extract infeasible ones; while dynamically explored ATGs can yield incomplete transitions. Recent learning-based approaches can treat ATG construction as a seed-supervised link-prediction task. However, the use of activity-layout and widget-trigger information for ATG construction remains limited. We propose ATGBuilder, a feature-assisted graph-learning approach for seed-supervised ATG construction. ATGBuilder uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to summarize UI activity metadata from layouts into compact textual functionality summaries. ATGBuilder explicitly models widget-trigger information into the edge attribute: It then uses an auxiliary widget-attribute reconstruction objective on this information during model training. ATGBuilder's performance was evaluated across a series of ablations on the frontmatter corpus, and an experiment on benchmark using manually-checked ground-truth ATGs. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ATGBuilder significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness by improving automated GUI exploration tools through better navigation guidance.

</details>


### [124] [On the Reproducibility of Quantum Software Defect Datasets: A Case Study of Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27124)
*Haruto Ohto,Yuta Ishimoto,Shinsuke Matsumoto,Shinji Kusumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对量子软件缺陷数据集Bugs4Q进行复制实验，发现其可复现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%降至v2.3.1的16.2%，93.6%的失败与依赖相关。与先前针对传统软件的研究不同，Bugs4Q的复现失败大多无法通过调整依赖版本解决，而需修改源代码。研究者因此构建了Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本，将可复现性提升至78.4%，强调了在快速演化的量子软件生态中持续维护数据集的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明传统软件缺陷数据集（如Defects4J）存在随时间的可复现性衰退问题，但该发现是否适用于量子软件缺陷数据集尚不清楚。为填补这一空白，作者对量子程序缺陷数据集Bugs4Q进行了复制研究。

Method: 对Bugs4Q数据集中的37个缺陷工件在21个Qiskit核心库版本上进行了77,700次量子程序执行，测量可复现率，并手工分析复现失败的根本原因。

Result: Bugs4Q的可复现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%下降到v2.3.1的16.2%；93.6%的失败与依赖相关；与传统软件不同，大多数失败无法仅通过调整依赖版本解决，需要修改源代码（如迁移导入路径和API调用）。构建的Bugs4Q-Robust将可复现性从16.2%提升至78.4%。

Conclusion: 量子软件缺陷数据集面临比传统软件更严重的可复现性衰退问题，且修复难度更大。快速演化的量子软件生态系统对数据集持续维护提出了更高要求。

Abstract: The reproducibility of software defect datasets is essential for obtaining reliable and comparable research results. Zhu et al. have shown that defect datasets such as Defects4J suffer from reproduction failures (i.e., reported bugs become non-reproducible) as time passes since their creation. However, it remains unclear whether these findings generalize to quantum software defect datasets. We therefore conduct a replication study of the prior work using Bugs4Q, a widely used dataset of real-world bugs in quantum programs. Our analysis includes 77,700 quantum program executions of 37 Bugs4Q artifacts across 21 core-library versions. The experimental results showed that the reproducibility of Bugs4Q dropped from 62.2% on Qiskit v0.20.1 to 16.2% on v2.3.1, the latest version as of April 1, 2026. A manual inspection of the root causes further indicated that 93.6% of the failures were dependency-related. While these findings are consistent with those of the prior work, we also observed differences. In particular, most reproduction failures in Bugs4Q cannot be resolved merely by adjusting dependency versions; instead, they require source-code modifications such as migrating import paths and API invocations. Based on this observation, we curated Bugs4Q-Robust, a patched version of Bugs4Q to restore reproducibility. Bugs4Q-Robust increases reproducibility from 16.2% to 78.4% on Qiskit v2.3.1. Our findings highlight the importance of continuous dataset maintenance in the rapidly evolving quantum software ecosystem.

</details>


### [125] [Smaller Models, Unexpected Costs: Trade-offs in LLM Quantization for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27205)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Giordano d'Aloisio,Max Hort,Luca Traini,Antinisca Di Marco,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM量化在自动程序修复任务中的效果进行了实证评估，发现量化虽能减少高达85%的内存占用，但会改变模型行为（修复不同的问题集）、增加推理时间和能耗，且48%的量化配置被严格支配，不存在普适最优的量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅用基准分数概括量化效果，掩盖了模型行为变化和非功能性开销（如时间、能耗），缺乏对复杂软件工程任务中量化影响的深入理解。

Method: 在HumanEval-Java和Defects4J两个APR基准上，对6个代表性LLM评估了13种量化配置（不同位宽、方法、目标组件），从有效性、内存占用、推理时间和能耗四个维度进行Pareto权衡分析。

Result: 量化和未量化模型修复的问题集重叠度低但修复数量相当；量化减少内存占用最高达85%，但增加了推理时间和能耗；48%的配置被严格支配；最优权衡依赖于模型架构和任务复杂度。

Conclusion: 不存在绝对优于其他方法的量化方案，量化带来的影响是多维度的权衡，需要根据具体模型架构和任务需求进行选择。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) are powerful toolsand have been increasingly adopted for complex software engineering tasks. As the number of parameters increases, results can often be improved, but this also imposes substantialmemory requirements. While quantization effectively reduces thememory footprint, its overall impact is often summarized onlyby benchmark scores, which mask changes in model behaviorand non-functional overheads. In this work, we conduct anempirical evaluation of LLM quantization using AutomatedProgram Repair (APR), a complex task in software engineering.We analyze 13 quantization configurations spanning differentbit-widths, methods, and target components (weights and KVcache) across six representative LLMs, evaluated on two APRbenchmarks (HumanEval-Java and Defects4J).
  Our findings reveal that base and quantized models can provide different sets of repaired problems with little overlap, whileretaining a comparable number of repaired problems. Althoughquantization successfully reduces memory footprints by up to85%, it increases both inference time and energy consumption,which we attribute to suboptimal hardware utilization. OurPareto trade-off analysis shows that 48% of the configurationsevaluated are strictly dominated by alternatives. Rather thanidentifying a superior quantization method, our findings highlightthat the trade-offs between effectiveness, memory footprint,and energy efficiency are sensitive to the underlying modelarchitecture and the complexity of the task.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27258)
*Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判了面向对象编程的核心原则——将系统功能按问题域中的个体进行划分，认为这一原则实际上导致了功能的碎片化和混淆，并提出通过放弃面向对象、采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法来解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向对象编程的核心原则（按问题域个体划分功能）已主导软件工程数十年，但后续发展（如设计模式、面向方面编程等）试图解决的碎片化和功能混淆问题，实际上是该原则本身的必然结果。因此需要重新审视这一基本原则。

Method: 通过批判性分析指出面向对象原则的内在缺陷，提出应放弃面向对象，采用一种将问题域中的个体与功能划分模块解耦的替代方法。

Result: 论文认为面向对象的功能划分既不自然也不直接，后续软件工程方法试图缓解的问题恰恰是该原则的必然产物。

Conclusion: 更简单的解决路径是回归原点，放弃面向对象，采用解耦问题域个体与功能模块的替代方案。

Abstract: A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代数据生成管道，通过分离级联线性特征来更好地解释和引导语言模型行为，并以消除谄媚行为为例，证明了该方法在特征解耦、检测和引导方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法依赖于简单的二元对比样本对，难以充分解耦模型特征，导致可解释性和引导效果受限。

Method: 提出级联数据生成管道，通过构建展现行为梯度变化的多级样本，而非简单二元对，来分离与行为线性相关的级联特征。

Result: 谄媚特征形成线性可分子空间；该方法在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导方面匹配或优于LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，且计算成本更低、可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 使用展现行为线性梯度变化的级联样本能够更好地解耦特征，提升模型行为的可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [128] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超越传统准确率指标的六维智能体性能评估框架，并以CORE-Bench Hard（科学代码计算可重复性基准）为案例，证明即使准确率饱和，这六个维度仍能提供有意义的性能洞察。作者发现了构造效度问题，改进了基准（CORE-Bench v1.1）并推出了OOD任务集，同时通过小规模随机实验发现人机协作可将速度提升约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估范式过度依赖准确率指标，当基准准确率饱和后通常直接替换为更难版本，而忽略了智能体性能的其他关键维度。作者希望提出一种更严格的替代评估范式，通过多维度分析来获取更多性能洞察。

Method: 1. 提出六维评估框架（构造效度、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与支架的相对重要性、人机协作提升）。2. 以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，分析其构造效度问题并改进为CORE-Bench v1.1，同时构建OOD任务集。3. 进行小规模随机实验测量人机协作的实际提升效果。

Result: 1. 发现了CORE-Bench Hard中的构造效度威胁（捷径等），改进后的基准（v1.1）和OOD任务集缓解了这些问题。2. 即使准确率饱和，CORE-Bench v1.1在衡量效率、可靠性、模型性能和支架性能方面仍然有效。3. 人机协作在真实计算可重复性任务上实现了约两倍的统计显著加速，且由于五分之一的人类单独复现因时间限制未完成，该加速效果可能被低估。

Conclusion: 准确率主导的评估范式存在局限性，多维度评估框架能够在准确率饱和后继续提供有价值的性能洞察，为智能体评估提供更严格的替代方案。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [129] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中，顺从人格方向与拒绝方向存在交互作用：顺从人格会门控（gating）拒绝行为。通过干预Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中的这两个方向，表明拒绝在后期表达阶段被人格门控，而非独立机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将拒绝方向和人格方向视为独立机制，但本文假设二者存在交互，即顺从人格会门控拒绝行为。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中提取顺从模型人格方向和拒绝方向，通过干预（steering/projecting）这两个方向，观察拒绝率变化，并在不同层进行实验。

Result: 顺从人格方向抑制拒绝：Llama中拒绝率从97%降至2%。在后期层重新引入拒绝方向可部分恢复拒绝，但早期层不行。在后期层窗口投影掉人格方向可恢复拒绝基线，随机方向则无效。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为在后期表达阶段被人格门控，位于拒绝计算的下游。将拒绝视为孤立方向会忽略其对人格的依赖性。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [130] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve，一种基于LLM驱动的进化框架，用于生成、评估和迭代改进可执行的交易策略。系统展现出涌现性的自适应策略逻辑，并引入元进化外层循环来优化内层循环的程序合成提示，在复杂金融环境中实现了优于人工设计指令的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM作为语义变异算子的进化发现范式从静态编码基准扩展到算法交易领域，该领域具有噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续等独特挑战，需要能够持续适应环境变化的策略生成方法。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve双循环进化框架：内层循环利用LLM作为语义变异算子生成、评估和迭代改进以Python代码表示的交易策略；外层循环对指导内层程序合成的提示进行元进化，自动发现更优的搜索启发式策略。

Result: 系统展现出涌现性的交易规则自主切换等自适应策略逻辑；元进化外层循环发现的搜索启发式在探索与利用之间取得更好平衡，减少了零交易失败次数，且一致优于初始人工设计的指令。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化为复杂环境下的持续程序合成提供了一种可行的方法，特别是在算法交易这类高度动态和不确定性的领域。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [131] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的比较分析流程，用于大规模研究AI智能体协议治理话语。通过对ERC-8004（去中心化）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种互操作性标准的4,323条治理记录分析，发现治理形式影响主题焦点，但两种体制在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上相似，而开放治理环境下话语一致性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI智能体协议日益增多，但塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证层面仍未被充分研究，缺乏大规模分析治理话语的方法论工具。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的比较分析流水线，整合自动标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对ERC-8004（无许可链上治理）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种协议共4,323条治理参与记录进行实证分析。

Result: 治理形式影响实质性主题焦点，但两种体制在参与不平等程度和社区碎片化程度上相当；在无许可环境下话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更大的主题趋同。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法能够推进技术治理的实证研究，为设计更公平的智能体AI标准提供了启示。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [132] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了Reddit、WebMD和FDA不良事件报告系统中关于九种抗抑郁药的466,525篇帖子、60,782条评论及二十年FAERS数据，通过保留来源信息的实体识别（F1最高0.973）和Neo4j图谱实现监管证据与患者经验的区隔化融合。研究发现患者社区数据（Jaccard相似度高达0.905）与监管报告形成独立安全信号，舍曲林的不良事件在社区中出现时间平均早于FDA记录数百天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者越来越多地在网上搜索药物信息，但精神科药物的安全性知识分裂为两类：权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录，以及贴近体验但未经验证的患者叙述。在精神医学领域，不当整合信息可能放大恐惧、引发反安慰剂效应和药物不依从，因此亟需一种既能融合两类数据又不混淆证据与轶事的方法。

Method: 提出了一种溯源感知的、基于知识图谱的多智能体框架：1) 收集9种抗抑郁药的466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论和二十年FAERS数据；2) 使用大语言模型实体识别管道（与医生标注进行基准对比）；3) 基于ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA术语构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留每条声明的来源信息。

Result: 1) LLM实体识别在药物和病症上的最高F1分数分别达到0.969和0.973；2) Reddit与WebMD之间的Jaccard相似度高达0.905，远高于二者与FAERS的一致性；3) 患者生成数据构成部分独立的药物安全信号；4) 舍曲林的许多不良事件在社区数据中出现时间比FDA记录早数百天。

Conclusion: 溯源感知的源数据整合为更可审计的精神科药物信息提供了可行路径，其有效性和患者获益有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [133] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出漂移扩散增强Elo评分系统（DD-Elo），通过融合认知神经科学的漂移扩散模型，利用棋局中的步级信息实现比传统Elo更快速、更灵敏的技能评估，同时保持理论一致性和向后兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在响应滞后问题，无法捕捉比赛过程中的细粒度质量信息，而直接将步级信息纳入评分调整面临噪声大、状态空间庞大的挑战。

Method: 借鉴认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM），将技能表达建模为决策过程，整合步级数据以捕捉技能的快速波动；通过严格数学推导证明DD-Elo与传统Elo的偏差有界。

Result: 大量实验表明DD-Elo在适应技能变化速度上优于传统Elo系统。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评分生态提供了一种可解释、高响应性且向后兼容的解决方案。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [134] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算治理模型，要求AI智能体在高风险行动（如临床处方、软件部署）前，必须获得多个独立权威来源的可验证证据，而非依赖对其推理过程的监控。智能体保留规划和推理的完全自主权，但无执行权；执行基于声明意图的密码学绑定、确定性策略评估和防篡改日志记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体可能执行不可逆的高风险行动（如临床处方、生产部署），而传统的人类机构治理模式并非通过监控推理过程，而是要求行动时提供独立可验证的证据。本文旨在将这一制度模式形式化为AI智能体系统的计算治理模型。

Method: 形式化了一种计算治理模型：智能体保留规划和推理的完全自主权，但对指定的高风险行动无执行权；执行取决于一系列前提条件，每个条件由独立权威来源单独证明，并与声明意图进行密码学绑定；由确定性策略评估；决策记录在防篡改日志中，可供独立重新验证。并提供了概念验证实现。

Result: 提出了一个计算治理模型，并通过软件部署和临床处方的示例展示了模型的应用，同时提供了概念验证实现。

Conclusion: 该模型通过分离自主规划与执行权限、引入多方独立证明和密码学绑定机制，为AI智能体在高风险场景下的安全可控执行提供了一种可行的治理框架。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [135] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动管道，通过自然语言生成折纸折痕图案，结合数学上的平面可折叠约束与自主美学评估循环，实现了满足多目标物理约束的AI辅助艺术设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在生成同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美学的物理艺术作品（如计算折纸）方面仍面临挑战。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: COrigami管道包括五个阶段：从自然语言生成语义简笔画、计算基础排布、求解平面可折叠折痕图案、对折叠后的图案进行塑形，以及通过自主美学评估循环驱动的强化学习来优化生成模型。

Result: 该系统作为高效的协作助手，生成的起点结构可供人类艺术家进一步拓展和塑造，实现了算法优化与自主美学批评的整合。

Conclusion: 本文展示了AI系统如何在满足多目标物理约束的前提下，实现可靠、基于数学基础的共创性，证明了AI能够处理严格几何约束与主观审美并存的艺术创作任务。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [136] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，随着代码智能体生成能力增强，验证比生成更难成为核心瓶颈。验证信号面临可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性三重挑战，且任何固定奖励函数在策略能力持续增长时都无法保持有效，验证必须与生成器协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成容易，但当前代码智能体的能力反转了这一直觉：生成复杂候选解已不再困难，可靠地验证它们反而成为更难的问题。每个验证器都只是人类意图的代理而非意图本身，这使得验证面临双重困难——意图本质上的欠指定性导致忠实检查困难，训练过程中的优化又扩大了代理与意图之间的差距（表现为奖励破解或信号饱和）。

Method: 从可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量，并提出三者兼顾是核心挑战。研究四种奖励构建方法：通用编码任务的测试验证器、前端任务的评分标准验证器、真实世界智能体任务中以用户为验证器、长程任务的自动化智能体验证器。在不同任务类型和策略能力水平上进行深入分析和实验。

Result: 有针对性的验证设计可以有效抑制奖励破解、提高任务完成质量，并在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著收益。实验表明，没有固定奖励函数能在策略能力持续增长时保持有效。

Conclusion: 验证必须与生成器协同进化，而非采用固定不变的验证方案。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [137] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了工具增强型LLM智能体在真实能源市场分析任务上的表现，构建了包含243个专家策展问题的三类评估环境，并设计了多维评估协议，填补了能源领域缺乏动态、多步推理评估基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准已覆盖金融、编程、法律、药物发现等领域，但能源领域评估仍局限于静态知识回忆。能源行业需要实时数据检索、专业监管与市场知识以及多步定量推理，这一关键缺口亟需填补。

Method: 构建243个专家策展问题，涵盖三类任务：市场数据检索与分析、知识检索与解释、高级量化建模与决策分析。配置可组合的领域工具集（包括实时电力市场API、监管档案检索、电费数据库、资产优化模型及RAG）。采用多维评估协议，从方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐度和来源有效性四个维度评分，并按类别路由匹配评分标准。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM进行了比较分析，揭示了模型能力与领域工具在高风险专业场景中的交互作用，并公开了关键工件以支持可复现性和后续研究。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建能源领域首个动态、多步推理的智能体评估基准，为评估和改进LLM在能源市场分析中的实际应用能力提供了系统框架，推动了该专业领域智能体系统的发展。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [138] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了提示组合型智能体系统中的"组合行为泄露"(CBL)问题——编辑一个提示模块会静默影响其他模块的行为，并通过可复用的三通道协议在部署的岗位评估智能体上验证了该现象，发现内容通道产生可检测的配对效应(Cohen's d=0.63)，但处于亚阈值状态，在标准QA中不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合型智能体系统在实践中存在一个反复出现的故障模式：编辑一个提示模块会静默改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的形式化定义和系统化测量方法。

Method: 提出了可复用的三通道协议：通过体积(volume)、内容(content)和形式(form)三个通道扰动非焦点模块，在部署的岗位评估智能体(Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144次试验)上进行探测。

Result: 仅内容通道产生可检测的配对效应(Cohen's d=0.63, bootstrap 95% CI排除零)；没有推荐结果发生翻转——处于标准QA不可见的亚阈值状态，但在部署智能体做出的数千次决策中会累积放大。

Conclusion: CBL与已知的智能体故障轴(对抗注入、认知退化、多智能体故障传播、隐私泄露)正交。本文提供了操作性定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统类特征，将跨模块干扰测量确立为提示组合型智能体评估的必要要求。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有MLLM评估基准局限于孤立任务，未能有效衡量跨模态信息整合能力，存在时空连贯性、物理世界理解、模态一致性和选择性注意力等关键评估缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能力快速提升，但评估方法发展滞后，现有基准局限于孤立任务，难以反映模型是否真正整合跨模态信息，亟需填补评估缺口以衡量多模态智能的真实进展。

Method: 通过审查当前MLLM评估手段并回顾现有基准分类体系，识别出评估缺口，包括时空连贯性、物理世界理解、模态一致性和选择性注意力四个维度。

Result: 发现现有评估基准存在以下关键缺口：(1) 时空连贯性，(2) 物理世界理解，(3) 模态一致性，(4) 选择性注意力。

Conclusion: 填补上述评估缺口对于衡量多模态智能的真实进展和揭示模型能力边界至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [140] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX是一个已在生产环境中部署的多智能体系统，通过四个紧密耦合的阶段（头脑风暴、开发、评估、进化）实现推荐算法实验的自主闭环运作，将人工驱动的推荐迭代转变为自我进化的自动化引擎，大幅提升实验规模与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐算法迭代从手工、依赖工程师的过程向工业化研究循环转型，但存在结构性执行瓶颈：从想法到上线仍依赖人工工程师生成假设、修改生产代码、启动A/B实验并归因线上结果。创新因此随人头数线性增长，而非随证据、算力和累积实验知识复合增长。

Method: AgentX由四个紧耦合阶段组成闭环：(1) Brainstorm Agent综合历史实验、系统架构、数据分析和外部研究证据，生成排序的可执行提案；(2) Developing Agent通过基于仓库的代码生成和多维可靠性验证，将提案转化为生产级代码；(3) Evaluation Agent进行安全在线部署，通过带护栏否决权的A/B判断，将成功和失败都转化为结构化知识资产；(4) Harness Evolution层（SGPO）将执行轨迹提炼为语义梯度更新，持续优化智能体自身，使系统实现自我改进。

Result: AgentX能够以任何手动工作流无法维持的规模和速度，自主生成、实施、评估并从推荐实验中学习，实现了从自动化到自我进化的跨越。

Conclusion: AgentX通过重构推荐系统迭代的生产函数，打破了人力瓶颈，使创新不再随人头数线性增长，而是能够随证据、算力和累积实验知识复合增长，代表了推荐算法工业化的重要进展。

Abstract: Recommendation algorithm iteration is moving from an artisanal, engineer-bound process toward an industrialized research loop, but this transition remains blocked by a structural execution bottleneck: the idea-to-launch cycle still depends on human engineers to generate hypotheses, modify production code, launch A/B experiments, and attribute online results. Innovation therefore scales linearly with headcount rather than compounding with evidence, compute, and accumulated experimental knowledge. We present AgentX, a production-deployed multi-agent system that fundamentally restructures this production function. AgentX operates as a self-evolving development engine: it autonomously generates, implements, evaluates, and learns from recommendation experiments at a scale and pace that no manual workflow can sustain.
  The system orchestrates four tightly coupled stages in a closed loop. A Brainstorm Agent synthesizes evidence from historical experiments, system architecture, data analysis, and external research into ranked, executable proposals. A Developing Agent translates each proposal into production-ready code through repository-grounded generation and multi-dimensional reliability verification. An Evaluation Agent conducts safe online rollout with guardrail-vetoed A/B judgment, converting both successes and failures into structured knowledge assets. A Harness Evolution layer (SGPO) then distills execution trajectories into semantic-gradient updates that continuously sharpen the agents themselves -- making the system not merely automated, but self-improving.

</details>


### [141] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym是一个统一量化金融Agent开发环境，覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多阶段任务，提供自动化任务构建、容器化运行、回测引擎等基础设施，支持SFT和RL后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化金融Agent评估分散在各孤立任务上，忽略了金融工作流的多阶段特性（预测、策略构建、风险管理、交易等），导致单一任务评估高估Agent能力，无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融意义决策方面的弱点。

Method: 提出OpenFinGym统一环境，包含：(1)覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易、欺诈检测的统一执行与验证接口；(2)自动化任务构建流水线，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(3)容器化运行时与主机端验证服务，支持可扩展Agent部署并防止训练测试泄漏；(4)低延迟数据流设计的回测引擎；(5)长周期和事件市场预测的延迟解决支持；(6)SFT和RL后训练集成。

Result: 构建了一个集成多阶段量化金融任务的统一平台，支持自动化任务生成和多种后训练范式。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一环境和自动化任务构建，弥补了现有量化金融Agent评估碎片化的不足，为多阶段金融工作流下的Agent开发提供了更全面的基础设施。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [142] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，库兹韦尔的加速回报理论主要适用于执行性和基础设施能力的指数级增长，但并不能解决科学发现的核心问题——即对当前框架结构是否充分进行定性推理的能力。基于ARC-AGI-3基准测试中人类与AI的巨大差距，论文将定性引擎科学（QES）定位为弥补这一缺失能力的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战库兹韦尔加速回报理论在科学发现领域的适用性，指出执行性能力的指数增长无法解决科学发现中需要的定性推理问题，并基于ARC-AGI-3基准测试中AI与人类的巨大差距，论证这一缺失能力的重要性。

Method: 通过数学化解释库兹韦尔的加速回报理论，分析其在执行性能力和科学发现中的不同适用性；利用ARC-AGI-3基准测试结果（人类天花板水平 vs. AI低于1%）作为实证支撑；引入定性引擎科学（QES）作为弥补该缺失能力的框架。

Result: 论证了加速回报理论无法解决科学发现的核心问题；ARC-AGI-3结果显示当前AI与人类灵活推理能力仍存在巨大鸿沟；QES被定位为应对这一缺失能力的方案。

Conclusion: 科学发现的过程本身是一种值得保存、组织和传播的人类智慧形式，QES的价值不依赖于通用人工智能何时到来，而在于保存这种智慧本身。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.
